// parseGitConfig parses the output of git config --null --get-regexp: one
// "key\nvalue" record per NUL. Later entries win, as in git.
func parseGitConfig(out []byte) Config {
        cfg := Config{}
        for rec := range bytes.SplitSeq(out, []byte{0}) {
                if len(rec) == 0 {
                        continue
                }
                key, value, _ := strings.Cut(string(rec), "\n")
                cfg[key] = value
        }
        return cfg
}

// Features returns the enabled delta features, highest priority first,
// following delta's rules: features from DELTA_FEATURES (env) and --features
// (flag) come first; [delta] features is used only when neither replaces it
// (a leading "+" in env adds instead). Each feature precedes the features it
// enables itself, and among the features of one setting the rightmost wins.
func Features(cfg Config, env, flag string) []string {
        var input []string
        useConfig := flag == ""
        switch {
        case strings.HasPrefix(env, "+"):
                input = append(strings.Fields(env[1:]), strings.Fields(flag)...)
        case env != "":
                input = strings.Fields(env)
                useConfig = false
        default:
                input = strings.Fields(flag)
        }

        var out []string
        var visit func(f string)
        visit = func(f string) {
                out = append(out, f)
                children := strings.Fields(cfg["delta."+f+".features"])
                for _, c := range slices.Backward(children) {
                        if !slices.Contains(out, c) {
                                visit(c)
                        }
                }
        }
        for _, f := range slices.Backward(input) {
                visit(f)
        }
        if useConfig {
                for _, f := range slices.Backward(strings.Fields(cfg["delta.features"])) {
                        visit(f)
                }
        }
        return out
}

// lookup returns the value of a delta option: the main [delta] section wins,
// then the features in priority order.
func lookup(cfg Config, features []string, key string) (string, bool) {
        if v, ok := cfg["delta."+key]; ok {
                return v, true
        }
        for _, f := range features {
                if v, ok := cfg["delta."+f+"."+key]; ok {
                        return v, true
                }
        }
        return "", false
}

package theme

import (
        "path/filepath"
        "reflect"
        "strings"

        "github.com/BurntSushi/toml"
)

// Kind is where a theme comes from.
type Kind int

const (
        KindBuiltin Kind = iota // jdiff's defaults
        KindGit                 // delta's and jdiff's git config sections
        KindFile                // a theme file
)

// Spec is a theme as its source defines it: the values as written, nil for
// options the source leaves unset. Resolve turns it into a Theme.
//
// The TOML keys are the option names; in the git config they are keys of
// the [delta] section, or of [jdiff] where the git tag says so.
type Spec struct {
        Name string `toml:"-"`
        Kind Kind   `toml:"-"`

        MinusStyle            *string `toml:"minus-style"`
        MinusEmphStyle        *string `toml:"minus-emph-style"`
        MinusNonEmphStyle     *string `toml:"minus-non-emph-style"` // unset: minus-style
        PlusStyle             *string `toml:"plus-style"`
        PlusEmphStyle         *string `toml:"plus-emph-style"`
        PlusNonEmphStyle      *string `toml:"plus-non-emph-style"` // unset: plus-style
        ZeroStyle             *string `toml:"zero-style"`
        LineNumbersMinusStyle *string `toml:"line-numbers-minus-style"`
        LineNumbersPlusStyle  *string `toml:"line-numbers-plus-style"`
        LineNumbersZeroStyle  *string `toml:"line-numbers-zero-style"`
        LineNumbersLeftStyle  *string `toml:"line-numbers-left-style"`
        LineNumbersRightStyle *string `toml:"line-numbers-right-style"`
        FileStyle             *string `toml:"file-style"`
        FileDecorationStyle   *string `toml:"file-decoration-style"` // only its color is used
        InlineHintStyle       *string `toml:"inline-hint-style"`
        FileAddedLabel        *string `toml:"file-added-label"`
        FileRemovedLabel      *string `toml:"file-removed-label"`
        FileModifiedLabel     *string `toml:"file-modified-label"`
        FileRenamedLabel      *string `toml:"file-renamed-label"`

        AnchorStyle             *string `toml:"anchor-style" git:"jdiff"`
        PendingStyle            *string `toml:"pending-style" git:"jdiff"`
        CursorStyle             *string `toml:"cursor-style" git:"jdiff"`
        FileBarStyle            *string `toml:"file-bar-style" git:"jdiff"`
        CommitBarStyle          *string `toml:"commit-bar-style" git:"jdiff"`
        CommitHashStyle         *string `toml:"commit-hash-style" git:"jdiff"`
        BranchStyle             *string `toml:"branch-style" git:"jdiff"`
        FillerStyle             *string `toml:"filler-style" git:"jdiff"`              // unset: file-decoration-style's color
        WhitespaceStyle         *string `toml:"whitespace-style" git:"jdiff"`          // unset: file-decoration-style's color
        TrailingWhitespaceStyle *string `toml:"trailing-whitespace-style" git:"jdiff"` // shown by t
        HiddenCharStyle         *string `toml:"hidden-char-style" git:"jdiff"`         // ☠ for invisible characters
        OverflowStyle           *string `toml:"overflow-style" git:"jdiff"`            // « » at the edges of cut lines
        MoreStyle               *string `toml:"more-style" git:"jdiff"`                // ↑ ↓ for items of lists out of view; unset: whitespace-style
        SearchStyle             *string `toml:"search-style" git:"jdiff"`              // text found by /
        MinusIndentStyle        *string `toml:"minus-indent-style" git:"jdiff"`        // unset: minus-emph-style
        PlusIndentStyle         *string `toml:"plus-indent-style" git:"jdiff"`         // unset: plus-emph-style
        MinusMovedStyle         *string `toml:"minus-moved-style" git:"jdiff"`         // removed lines that moved elsewhere
        PlusMovedStyle          *string `toml:"plus-moved-style" git:"jdiff"`          // added lines that moved here
        DocHeadingStyle         *string `toml:"doc-heading-style" git:"jdiff"`         // headings of --readme; unset: branch-style
        DocCodeStyle            *string `toml:"doc-code-style" git:"jdiff"`            // code in --readme; unset: file-style
        DocLinkStyle            *string `toml:"doc-link-style" git:"jdiff"`            // links in --readme

        // Colors are named colors that styles can use like color names.
        Colors map[string]string `toml:"colors"`
}

// options calls f with the key, git config section and field of each option
// of s.
func (s *Spec) options(f func(key, section string, field **string)) {
        v := reflect.ValueOf(s).Elem()
        for i := range v.NumField() {
                key := v.Type().Field(i).Tag.Get("toml")
                if key == "-" || key == "colors" {
                        continue
                }
                section := v.Type().Field(i).Tag.Get("git")
                if section == "" {
                        section = "delta"
                }
                f(key, section, v.Field(i).Addr().Interface().(**string))
        }
}

// isJdiffKey reports whether key is a [jdiff] key rather than a delta one.
func isJdiffKey(key string) bool {
        jdiff := false
        (&Spec{}).options(func(k, section string, _ **string) {
                jdiff = jdiff || (k == key && section == "jdiff")
        })
        return jdiff
}

// Builtin returns jdiff's default theme. Its colors are made for 16 color
// mode: unlike delta's defaults they are foreground colors only, since ANSI
// backgrounds are too loud for whole lines.
func Builtin() Spec {
        str := func(s string) *string { return &s }
        return Spec{
                Name: "default",
                Kind: KindBuiltin,

                MinusStyle:            str("red"),
                MinusEmphStyle:        str("bright-red bold"),
                PlusStyle:             str("green"),
                PlusEmphStyle:         str("bright-green bold"),
                ZeroStyle:             str("normal"),
                LineNumbersMinusStyle: str("red"),
                LineNumbersPlusStyle:  str("green"),
                LineNumbersZeroStyle:  str("normal"),
                LineNumbersLeftStyle:  str("blue"),
                LineNumbersRightStyle: str("blue"),
                FileStyle:             str("blue"),
                FileDecorationStyle:   str("blue ul"),
                InlineHintStyle:       str("blue"),
                FileAddedLabel:        str("added:"),
                FileRemovedLabel:      str("removed:"),
                FileModifiedLabel:     str(""),
                FileRenamedLabel:      str("renamed:"),

                AnchorStyle:  str("yellow bold"),
                PendingStyle: str("magenta bold"),
                CursorStyle:  str("reverse bold"),
                // Bars with a background, so file and commit borders stand out.
                FileBarStyle:    str("bold bright-white blue"),
                CommitBarStyle:  str("bold black yellow"),
                CommitHashStyle: str("yellow"),
                BranchStyle:     str("yellow bold"),
                // Moved code as git colors it by default.
                MinusMovedStyle: str("magenta"),
                PlusMovedStyle:  str("cyan"),
                // Trailing whitespace as git marks whitespace errors.
                TrailingWhitespaceStyle: str("normal red"),
                HiddenCharStyle:         str("normal red"),
                OverflowStyle:           str("blue"),
                SearchStyle:             str("black yellow"),
                // Links as browsers draw them.
                DocLinkStyle: str("bright-blue bold"),
        }
}

// FromGit returns the theme that delta's and jdiff's git config sections in
// cfg define with the given features (see Features): an option in [delta]
// wins over the features in their order, jdiff's options are read from
// [jdiff]. As in delta, any key of [delta] can name a color.
func FromGit(cfg Config, features []string) Spec {
        s := Spec{Name: "delta", Kind: KindGit, Colors: map[string]string{}}
        s.options(func(key, section string, field **string) {
                if section == "jdiff" {
                        if v, ok := cfg["jdiff."+key]; ok {
                                *field = &v
                        }
                } else if v, ok := lookup(cfg, features, key); ok {
                        *field = &v
                }
        })
        for k, v := range cfg {
                if name, ok := strings.CutPrefix(k, "delta."); ok && !strings.Contains(name, ".") {
                        s.Colors[name] = v
                }
        }
        return s
}

// FromFile reads the theme file path, a TOML file of options and a [colors]
// table, and names the theme after the file. It also returns the keys of the
// file that are no options.
func FromFile(path string) (Spec, []string, error) {
        s := Spec{Name: strings.TrimSuffix(filepath.Base(path), ".toml"), Kind: KindFile}
        md, err := toml.DecodeFile(path, &s)
        if err != nil {
                return Spec{}, nil, err
        }
        var unknown []string
        for _, k := range md.Undecoded() {
                unknown = append(unknown, k.String())
        }
        // Color words are matched lowercased, as git lowercases its keys.
        colors := make(map[string]string, len(s.Colors))
        for name, c := range s.Colors {
                colors[strings.ToLower(name)] = c
        }
        s.Colors = colors
        return s, unknown, nil
}

package theme

import (
        "fmt"
        "image/color"
        "strconv"
        "strings"

        "charm.land/lipgloss/v2"
)

// ansiNames are delta's names for the 16 ANSI colors.
var ansiNames = map[string]int{
        "black": 0, "red": 1, "green": 2, "yellow": 3,
        "blue": 4, "magenta": 5, "purple": 5, "cyan": 6, "white": 7,
}

// ParseStyle parses a delta style string: attributes, then an optional
// foreground and background color. Color words that are neither a known
// color nor "normal" are looked up in named, like delta does.
// Words that only matter to delta (omit, raw, box, ...) are ignored.
func ParseStyle(s string, named map[string]string) (lipgloss.Style, error) {
        style := lipgloss.NewStyle()
        colors := 0
        for _, word := range strings.Fields(strings.ToLower(s)) {
                word = strings.Trim(word, `"'`)
                switch word {
                case "bold":
                        style = style.Bold(true)
                case "dim":
                        style = style.Faint(true)
                case "italic":
                        style = style.Italic(true)
                case "ul", "underline":
                        style = style.Underline(true)
                case "blink":
                        style = style.Blink(true)
                case "reverse":
                        style = style.Reverse(true)
                case "strike":
                        style = style.Strikethrough(true)
                case "hidden", "omit", "raw", "line-number", "file", "omit-code-fragment", "box", "ol", "none":
                default:
                        c, err := parseColor(word, named)
                        if err != nil {
                                return lipgloss.NewStyle(), fmt.Errorf("style %q: %w", s, err)
                        }
                        switch colors {
                        case 0:
                                if c != nil {
                                        style = style.Foreground(c)
                                }
                        case 1:
                                if c != nil {
                                        style = style.Background(c)
                                }
                        default:
                                return lipgloss.NewStyle(), fmt.Errorf("style %q: more than two colors", s)
                        }
                        colors++
                }
        }
        return style, nil
}

// parseColor returns the color for word, or nil for no color. Colors beyond
// the 16 ANSI ones are passed on as-is; the 16 color profile of the program
// maps them to the nearest ANSI color when it draws.
func parseColor(word string, named map[string]string) (color.Color, error) {
        switch word {
        case "normal", "auto", "syntax":
                return nil, nil
        }
        if n, ok := ansiNames[word]; ok {
                return lipgloss.Color(strconv.Itoa(n)), nil
        }
        if base, ok := strings.CutPrefix(word, "bright"); ok {
                if n, ok := ansiNames[strings.TrimPrefix(base, "-")]; ok {
                        return lipgloss.Color(strconv.Itoa(n + 8)), nil
                }
        }
        if n, err := strconv.Atoi(word); err == nil && n >= 0 && n <= 255 {
                return lipgloss.Color(word), nil
        }
        if isHexColor(word) {
                return lipgloss.Color(word), nil
        }
        if v, ok := named[word]; ok && !strings.HasPrefix(word, "delta.") {
                // A named color defined elsewhere in the config; no further nesting.
                return parseColor(strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(v)), nil)
        }
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown color %q", word)
}

func isHexColor(s string) bool {
        if len(s) != 7 || s[0] != '#' {
                return false
        }
        _, err := strconv.ParseUint(s[1:], 16, 32)
        return err == nil
}

// Package theme builds jdiff's styles. A theme comes from a source, as a
// Spec: jdiff's defaults, delta's git config settings or a theme file;
// Resolve turns it into the styles, with the defaults for what it leaves
// unset.
//
// jdiff renders in 16 color mode: every color is an ANSI color, so the
// terminal palette decides the actual colors. Hex and 256 colors in a theme
// are mapped to the nearest ANSI color.
package theme

import (
        "fmt"
        "slices"

        "charm.land/lipgloss/v2"
)

// Theme holds all styles of the jdiff views.
type Theme struct {
        Minus, MinusNonEmph, MinusEmph, MinusIndent lipgloss.Style
        Plus, PlusNonEmph, PlusEmph, PlusIndent     lipgloss.Style
        Zero                                        lipgloss.Style

        NumMinus, NumPlus, NumZero lipgloss.Style
        NumLeft, NumRight          lipgloss.Style // separators after the numbers

        Title      lipgloss.Style // file names
        Decoration lipgloss.Style // pane separator
        Hint       lipgloss.Style // status bar

        Anchor, Pending, Cursor, Filler lipgloss.Style
        Whitespace                      lipgloss.Style // unchanged whitespace when shown
        TrailingWhitespace              lipgloss.Style // whitespace at the end of lines when shown
        HiddenChar                      lipgloss.Style // ☠ for invisible and direction changing characters
        Overflow                        lipgloss.Style // « and » at the edges of cut lines
        More                            lipgloss.Style // ↑ and ↓ for items of lists out of view
        Search                          lipgloss.Style // text found by /
        MinusMoved, PlusMoved           lipgloss.Style // moved code: removed here, added there
        FileBar, CommitBar              lipgloss.Style // flow mode: start of a file, a commit
        CommitHash                      lipgloss.Style // commit hashes in the log
        Branch                          lipgloss.Style // branch above the commit list
        DocHeading, DocCode, DocLink    lipgloss.Style // --readme: headings, code, links

        AddedLabel, RemovedLabel, ModifiedLabel, RenamedLabel string

        // Warnings lists settings that could not be used.
        Warnings []string
}

// Default returns the theme without any configuration.
func Default() *Theme { return Resolve(Builtin()) }

// Resolve builds the theme that sp defines. Options sp leaves unset or sets
// to an invalid value take their default; invalid values are reported in
// Warnings.
func Resolve(sp Spec) *Theme {
        def := Builtin()
        t := &Theme{}
        name := func(key string) string {
                switch {
                case sp.Kind == KindFile:
                        return sp.Name + ".toml: " + key
                case isJdiffKey(key):
                        return "jdiff." + key
                }
                return "delta." + key
        }
        val := func(v, d *string) string {
                if v != nil {
                        return *v
                }
                return *d
        }
        warn := func(key string, err error) {
                // Options that default to another one may report its error again.
                if w := fmt.Sprintf("%s: %v", name(key), err); !slices.Contains(t.Warnings, w) {
                        t.Warnings = append(t.Warnings, w)
                }
        }
        parse := func(key, s, fallback string) lipgloss.Style {
                st, err := ParseStyle(s, sp.Colors)
                if err != nil {
                        warn(key, err)
                        st, _ = ParseStyle(fallback, nil)
                }
                return st
        }
        // style returns option key with value v and default d.
        style := func(key string, v, d *string) lipgloss.Style {
                if v != nil {
                        return parse(key, *v, *d)
                }
                return parse(key, *d, "")
        }
        // styleOr returns option key with value v, which defaults to option
        // fbKey with value fv and default fd.
        styleOr := func(key string, v *string, fbKey string, fv, fd *string) lipgloss.Style {
                if v != nil {
                        return parse(key, *v, val(fv, fd))
                }
                return style(fbKey, fv, fd)
        }
        // styleOrStyle returns option key with value v, which defaults to the
        // style d, also when it is invalid.
        styleOrStyle := func(key string, v *string, d lipgloss.Style) lipgloss.Style {
                if v == nil {
                        return d
                }
                st, err := ParseStyle(*v, sp.Colors)
                if err != nil {
                        warn(key, err)
                        return d
                }
                return st
        }

        t.Minus = style("minus-style", sp.MinusStyle, def.MinusStyle)
        t.MinusEmph = style("minus-emph-style", sp.MinusEmphStyle, def.MinusEmphStyle)
        t.Plus = style("plus-style", sp.PlusStyle, def.PlusStyle)
        t.PlusEmph = style("plus-emph-style", sp.PlusEmphStyle, def.PlusEmphStyle)
        t.MinusNonEmph = styleOr("minus-non-emph-style", sp.MinusNonEmphStyle, "minus-style", sp.MinusStyle, def.MinusStyle)
        t.PlusNonEmph = styleOr("plus-non-emph-style", sp.PlusNonEmphStyle, "plus-style", sp.PlusStyle, def.PlusStyle)
        t.Zero = style("zero-style", sp.ZeroStyle, def.ZeroStyle)

        t.NumMinus = style("line-numbers-minus-style", sp.LineNumbersMinusStyle, def.LineNumbersMinusStyle)
        t.NumPlus = style("line-numbers-plus-style", sp.LineNumbersPlusStyle, def.LineNumbersPlusStyle)
        t.NumZero = style("line-numbers-zero-style", sp.LineNumbersZeroStyle, def.LineNumbersZeroStyle)
        t.NumLeft = style("line-numbers-left-style", sp.LineNumbersLeftStyle, def.LineNumbersLeftStyle)
        t.NumRight = style("line-numbers-right-style", sp.LineNumbersRightStyle, def.LineNumbersRightStyle)

        // Only the color of the decoration is used: jdiff draws no underlines.
        decoration := style("file-decoration-style", sp.FileDecorationStyle, def.FileDecorationStyle)
        t.Title = style("file-style", sp.FileStyle, def.FileStyle)
        t.Decoration = lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(decoration.GetForeground())
        t.Hint = style("inline-hint-style", sp.InlineHintStyle, def.InlineHintStyle)

        t.AddedLabel = val(sp.FileAddedLabel, def.FileAddedLabel)
        t.RemovedLabel = val(sp.FileRemovedLabel, def.FileRemovedLabel)
        t.ModifiedLabel = val(sp.FileModifiedLabel, def.FileModifiedLabel)
        t.RenamedLabel = val(sp.FileRenamedLabel, def.FileRenamedLabel)

        t.Anchor = style("anchor-style", sp.AnchorStyle, def.AnchorStyle)
        t.Pending = style("pending-style", sp.PendingStyle, def.PendingStyle)
        t.Cursor = style("cursor-style", sp.CursorStyle, def.CursorStyle)
        t.FileBar = style("file-bar-style", sp.FileBarStyle, def.FileBarStyle)
        t.CommitBar = style("commit-bar-style", sp.CommitBarStyle, def.CommitBarStyle)
        t.CommitHash = style("commit-hash-style", sp.CommitHashStyle, def.CommitHashStyle)
        t.Branch = style("branch-style", sp.BranchStyle, def.BranchStyle)
        t.MinusMoved = style("minus-moved-style", sp.MinusMovedStyle, def.MinusMovedStyle)
        t.PlusMoved = style("plus-moved-style", sp.PlusMovedStyle, def.PlusMovedStyle)
        t.Filler = styleOrStyle("filler-style", sp.FillerStyle, t.Decoration)
        t.Whitespace = styleOrStyle("whitespace-style", sp.WhitespaceStyle, t.Decoration)
        t.TrailingWhitespace = style("trailing-whitespace-style", sp.TrailingWhitespaceStyle, def.TrailingWhitespaceStyle)
        t.HiddenChar = style("hidden-char-style", sp.HiddenCharStyle, def.HiddenCharStyle)
        t.Overflow = style("overflow-style", sp.OverflowStyle, def.OverflowStyle)
        t.More = styleOrStyle("more-style", sp.MoreStyle, t.Whitespace)
        t.Search = style("search-style", sp.SearchStyle, def.SearchStyle)
        // Changed whitespace is drawn as · and →, by default like changed words.
        t.MinusIndent = styleOrStyle("minus-indent-style", sp.MinusIndentStyle, t.MinusEmph)
        t.PlusIndent = styleOrStyle("plus-indent-style", sp.PlusIndentStyle, t.PlusEmph)
        t.DocHeading = styleOrStyle("doc-heading-style", sp.DocHeadingStyle, t.Branch)
        t.DocCode = styleOrStyle("doc-code-style", sp.DocCodeStyle, t.Title)
        t.DocLink = style("doc-link-style", sp.DocLinkStyle, def.DocLinkStyle)
        return t
}

package ui

import (
        "fmt"
        "slices"
        "strings"

        tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
        "charm.land/lipgloss/v2"
        "github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"

        "jdiff/internal/config"
        "jdiff/internal/diff"
        "jdiff/internal/gitsrc"
        "jdiff/internal/notify"
        "jdiff/internal/source"
        "jdiff/internal/theme"
)

// entry is one file in the selector. Its diff is created with the selector,
// since moved code is searched in all files together, and its diff view on
// first open; both are kept, so anchors survive going back to the list.
type entry struct {
        source.File
        added, removed int
        diff           *fileDiff
        view           *Model
}

// label returns the status label, using delta's file labels.
func (e *entry) label(th *theme.Theme) string {
        switch {
        case e.OldPath != "":
                return th.RenamedLabel
        case !e.InOld:
                return th.AddedLabel
        case !e.InNew:
                return th.RemovedLabel
        }
        return th.ModifiedLabel
}

// name returns the path shown for the file, "old → new" for renames,
// printable: paths may hold control characters.
func (e *entry) name() string {
        if e.OldPath != "" {
                return printable(e.OldPath) + " → " + printable(e.Path)
        }
        return printable(e.Path)
}

// paneNames returns the names shown above the panes of the file's diff.
func (e *entry) paneNames() [2]string {
        return [2]string{"a/" + e.PathInOld(), "b/" + e.Path}
}

// refresh reloads the sides of e that changed on disk, see refreshFile. Its
// diff view and after, unless nil, follow each reload of its diff.
func (e *entry) refresh(after func(*fileDiff)) string {
        if e.Binary {
                return ""
        }
        msg := refreshFile(&e.File, e.diff, func() { e.followReload(after) })
        if msg != "" {
                e.updateStats()
        }
        return msg
}

// update takes f, the file listed again, into e, reloading the sides of
// its diff whose content changed. Its diff view and after, unless nil,
// follow each reload, as in refresh. It returns the anchors dropped and
// whether the content changed.
func (e *entry) update(f source.File, after func(*fileDiff)) (dropped int, changed bool) {
        was := [2][]string{e.Old, e.New}
        e.File = f
        if e.Binary {
                return 0, false
        }
        e.diff.names = e.paneNames()
        for s, lines := range [2][]string{f.Old, f.New} {
                if slices.Equal(was[s], lines) {
                        continue
                }
                changed = true
                dropped += e.diff.reload(side(s), lines)
                e.followReload(after)
        }
        return dropped, changed
}

// followReload lets its diff view and after, unless nil, follow a reload of
// its diff.
func (e *entry) followReload(after func(*fileDiff)) {
        if e.view != nil {
                e.view.syncLayout()
        }
        if after != nil {
                after(e.diff)
        }
}

func (e *entry) updateStats() {
        if e.Binary {
                return
        }
        if e.diff != nil {
                e.added, e.removed = diff.Stats(e.diff.rows)
                return
        }
        e.added, e.removed = diff.Stats(diff.Align(e.Old, e.New, nil, diff.Options{}))
}

// fileListRows is the most rows the file list takes while the preview shows,
// so that on a tall screen the preview gets the rest.
const fileListRows = 15

// App is the Bubble Tea model of the file selector. It shows the diff view
// of the selected file and returns to the list on backspace. A single file
// is opened directly.
type App struct {
        cfg    *config.Config
        commit *gitsrc.Commit // whose files are shown, nil outside the log
        files  []*entry
        relist ListFunc // lists the files again, nil when they cannot be
        // gitDiff runs git diff with arguments, nil outside jdiff diff; diffArgs
        // are those of the files shown.
        gitDiff  DiffFunc
        diffArgs []string
        // notify reports changes to the files on disk; nil while they are
        // checked every watch_interval, ticking while such a check is pending.
        notify  *notify.Watcher
        ticking bool
        list
        // previewTop is the first screen line of the preview scrolled by pgup
        // and pgdn, while previewFor is the file under the cursor; moving the
        // cursor drops it.
        previewFor *entry
        previewTop int
        open       *entry // file whose diff view is shown, nil for the list
        flow       *Flow  // flow view outside the log, created on first use
        msg        string
        prefix     string // "'" while waiting for the letter of a mark
}

// NewApp returns the file selector for files.
func NewApp(cfg *config.Config, files []source.File) *App { return newApp(cfg, files, nil) }

// ListFunc lists the files of a comparison again.
type ListFunc func() ([]source.File, error)

// DiffFunc returns the files git diff compares with args.
type DiffFunc func(args []string) ([]source.File, error)

// WithGitDiff makes a the files of git diff with args, which r runs again,
// and returns a.
func (a *App) WithGitDiff(args []string, gitDiff DiffFunc) *App {
        a.gitDiff, a.diffArgs = gitDiff, args
        a.relist = func() ([]source.File, error) { return a.gitDiff(a.diffArgs) }
        return a
}

// WithRelist sets how r lists the files again and returns a.
func (a *App) WithRelist(list ListFunc) *App {
        a.relist = list
        return a
}

// newApp returns the file selector for files, as part of the log for the
// files of commit when it is set.
func newApp(cfg *config.Config, files []source.File, commit *gitsrc.Commit) *App {
        a := &App{cfg: cfg, commit: commit}
        // The files are searched for moved code together.
        group := &moveGroup{}
        for _, f := range files {
                a.addEntry(group, &entry{File: f})
        }
        a.list = list{n: len(a.files), preview: &cfg.Settings.Preview, maxRows: fileListRows}
        if len(files) == 0 {
                a.msg = "no changed files"
        }
        if w := cfg.Warning(); w != "" {
                a.msg = w
        }
        if a.single() {
                a.openFile()
        }
        return a
}

// addEntry adds e to the files and its diff, unless binary, to group, the
// files searched for moved code together.
func (a *App) addEntry(group *moveGroup, e *entry) {
        if !e.Binary {
                group.add(a.diffOf(e), [2]string{e.PathInOld(), e.Path})
        }
        e.updateStats()
        a.files = append(a.files, e)
}

// stop stops watching the files: the view is replaced.
func (a *App) stop() {
        if a.notify != nil {
                a.notify.Close()
        }
}

// diffCommand returns the git diff command line of args.
func diffCommand(args []string) string {
        return strings.TrimSpace("git diff " + strings.Join(args, " "))
}

// inLog reports whether a is part of the log: backspace and N/P go to the
// log.
func (a *App) inLog() bool { return a.commit != nil }

// single reports whether there is exactly one file and it can be shown.
func (a *App) single() bool { return len(a.files) == 1 && !a.files[0].Binary }

// openPath opens the file with the given path and reports whether it could.
func (a *App) openPath(path string) bool {
        i := a.fileByPath(path)
        if i >= 0 {
                a.openIndex(i)
        }
        return i >= 0
}

// fileByPath returns the index of the file with the given path that can be
// shown, or -1.
func (a *App) fileByPath(path string) int {
        return slices.IndexFunc(a.files, func(e *entry) bool { return e.Path == path && !e.Binary })
}

// fileByGroup returns the index of the file at index i of the files searched
// for moved code, or -1.
func (a *App) fileByGroup(i int) int {
        return slices.IndexFunc(a.files, func(e *entry) bool { return e.diff != nil && e.diff.groupIndex == i })
}

// openIndex opens file i, after updating the counts of the file it leaves.
func (a *App) openIndex(i int) {
        if a.open != nil {
                a.open.updateStats()
        }
        a.cursor = i
        a.scrollToCursor()
        a.openFile()
}

// setMsg shows msg in the status bar of whatever is displayed.
func (a *App) setMsg(msg string) {
        switch {
        case a.flowShown():
                a.flow.msg = msg
        case a.open != nil:
                a.open.view.msg = msg
        default:
                a.msg = msg
        }
}

// Init starts watching the files, outside the log.
func (a *App) Init() tea.Cmd {
        if a.inLog() {
                return nil
        }
        return a.watch()
}

// watch starts watching the files on disk anew, by inotify when it can, and
// returns the command waiting for their changes. A pending check of polling
// is not started twice.
func (a *App) watch() tea.Cmd {
        if a.notify != nil {
                a.notify.Close()
        }
        var paths []string
        for _, e := range a.files {
                paths = append(paths, diskPaths(e.File)...)
        }
        w, cmd, msg := startWatching(a.cfg, a, paths)
        a.notify = w
        if msg != "" {
                a.setMsg(msg)
        }
        if w == nil && cmd != nil {
                if a.ticking {
                        return nil
                }
                a.ticking = true
        }
        return cmd
}

// refresh reloads the files i that changed on disk for which which holds.
func (a *App) refresh(which func(i int) bool) {
        var msgs []string
        reload := func(after func(*fileDiff)) {
                for i := range a.files {
                        if !which(i) {
                                continue
                        }
                        if msg := a.files[i].refresh(after); msg != "" {
                                msgs = append(msgs, msg)
                        }
                }
        }
        if a.flowShown() {
                a.flow.refresh(reload)
        } else {
                reload(nil)
        }
        if len(msgs) > 0 {
                a.setMsg(strings.Join(msgs, "; "))
        }
}

// relistFiles lists the files again (r). New files are added and gone ones
// dropped; a file still there keeps its entry, and so its diff with anchors
// and folds, reloaded where its content changed. A file that became binary
// or stopped being binary counts as gone and new. The cursor stays on its
// file; when the file shown is gone, the list is shown. The files are
// watched anew; it returns the command waiting for their changes. With quiet,
// it does not say when nothing changed.
func (a *App) relistFiles(quiet bool) tea.Cmd {
        if a.relist == nil {
                a.setMsg("nothing to load again")
                return nil
        }
        files, err := a.relist()
        if err != nil {
                a.setMsg(firstLine(err))
                return nil
        }
        kept := map[string]*entry{}
        for _, e := range a.files {
                kept[e.Path] = e
        }
        var cur *entry
        if a.cursor < len(a.files) {
                cur = a.files[a.cursor]
        }
        added, changed, dropped := 0, 0, 0
        update := func(after func(*fileDiff)) {
                old := a.files
                a.files = nil
                // The files are searched for moved code together.
                group := &moveGroup{}
                for _, f := range files {
                        e := kept[f.Path]
                        if e == nil || e.Binary != f.Binary {
                                e = &entry{File: f}
                                added++
                        } else {
                                delete(kept, f.Path)
                                n, ok := e.update(f, after)
                                if ok {
                                        changed++
                                }
                                dropped += n
                        }
                        a.addEntry(group, e)
                }
                if a.flow != nil {
                        a.flow.followFiles(0, old, a.files)
                }
        }
        shown := a.flowShown()
        if shown {
                a.flow.refresh(update)
        } else {
                update(nil)
        }
        a.list.n = len(a.files)
        if i := slices.Index(a.files, cur); i >= 0 {
                a.cursor = i
        } else {
                a.cursor = max(min(a.cursor, len(a.files)-1), 0)
        }
        msg := refreshed(added, len(kept), changed, dropped)
        switch {
        case len(a.files) == 0:
                a.open = nil
        case shown:
                if _, file, _ := a.flow.position(); file >= 0 {
                        a.cursor = file
                }
                a.open = a.files[a.cursor]
        case a.open != nil && !slices.Contains(a.files, a.open):
                msg = a.open.Path + " is gone; " + msg
                a.open = nil
        }
        a.scrollToCursor()
        if !quiet || msg != nothingChanged {
                a.setMsg(msg)
        }
        if a.inLog() {
                return nil
        }
        return a.watch()
}

// flowShown reports whether the flow view is shown instead of a diff view.
// Inside the log, the log shows its own flow view.
func (a *App) flowShown() bool { return a.open != nil && a.cfg.Settings.Flow && !a.inLog() }

func (a *App) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
        switch msg := msg.(type) {
        case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
                a.width, a.height = msg.Width, msg.Height
                a.scrollToCursor()
        case settingChangedMsg:
                a.scrollToCursor() // the preview takes room from the list
                if msg.problem != "" && a.open == nil {
                        a.msg = msg.problem
                }
        case watchTickMsg:
                if msg.owner != nil && msg.owner != a {
                        return a, nil
                }
                a.ticking = false
                if a.notify != nil {
                        return a, nil // inotify took over
                }
                // Polling checks the current file and its neighbors.
                a.refresh(func(i int) bool { return i >= a.cursor-1 && i <= a.cursor+1 })
                a.ticking = true
                return a, watchTick(a.cfg, a)
        case changedMsg:
                if msg.w != a.notify {
                        return a, nil
                }
                changed := func(i int) bool {
                        return slices.ContainsFunc(diskPaths(a.files[i].File), func(p string) bool { return slices.Contains(msg.paths, p) })
                }
                a.refresh(changed)
                return a, waitChanges(a.notify)
        case jumpMsg:
                a.jump(msg)
                return a, nil
        case markJumpMsg:
                a.gotoMark(msg.letter, msg.screenRow)
                return a, nil
        case switchFileMsg:
                a.switchFile(int(msg))
                return a, nil
        case toggleFlowMsg:
                a.toggleFlow()
                if w := saveState(a.cfg); w != "" {
                        a.setMsg(w)
                }
                return a, nil
        case backMsg: // from the flow view
                a.open = nil
                return a, nil
        case refreshMsg:
                return a, a.relistFiles(msg.quiet)
        case showMsg:
                a.setMsg(string(msg))
                return a, nil
        case toggleUntrackedMsg:
                if a.gitDiff == nil {
                        a.setMsg(untrackedOnlyInDiff)
                        return a, nil
                }
                a.cfg.Settings.Untracked = !a.cfg.Settings.Untracked // not saved
                return a, a.relistFiles(false)
        }
        if a.flowShown() {
                _, cmd := a.flow.Update(msg)
                if _, file, _ := a.flow.position(); file >= 0 {
                        a.cursor = file
                        a.scrollToCursor()
                }
                return a, cmd
        }
        if a.open != nil {
                if k, ok := msg.(tea.KeyPressMsg); ok && keyFile.key(diffView, k.String()).action == "to_files" {
                        a.open.updateStats()
                        a.open = nil
                        if a.inLog() && a.single() {
                                return a, func() tea.Msg { return backMsg{} }
                        }
                        return a, nil
                }
                _, cmd := a.open.view.Update(msg)
                return a, cmd
        }
        if k, ok := msg.(tea.KeyPressMsg); ok {
                return a, a.key(k.String())
        }
        return a, nil
}

func (a *App) key(k string) tea.Cmd {
        a.msg = ""
        cursor := a.cursor
        defer func() {
                if a.cursor != cursor {
                        a.previewFor = nil // another file shows its preview from the start
                }
        }()
        if a.prefix != "" {
                a.prefix = ""
                if isMarkLetter(k) {
                        a.gotoMark(k, (a.height-2)/2)
                }
                return nil
        }
        h := keyFile.key(fileList, k)
        page := max(a.listHeight()-1, 1)
        if delta, paging, ok := motion(h, page, len(a.files)); ok {
                if h := a.boxHeight(); paging && h > 0 && a.cursor < len(a.files) {
                        // The preview scrolls, by its height less a line, as the list pages.
                        a.scrollPreview(direction(delta > 0) * max(h-3, 1))
                        return nil
                }
                a.moveCursor(delta)
                return nil
        }
        switch h.action {
        case "quit":
                return tea.Quit
        case "open":
                if a.inLog() && a.cfg.Settings.Flow && a.cursor < len(a.files) {
                        file := a.cursor
                        return func() tea.Msg { return flowAtMsg(file) }
                }
                a.openFile()
        case "to_commits":
                if a.inLog() {
                        return func() tea.Msg { return backMsg{} }
                }
        case "history":
                if a.inLog() {
                        return switchCommit(h)
                }
        case "flow":
                return toggleFlow
        case "jump":
                a.prefix, a.msg = h.action, "jump to mark: press its letter"
        default:
                return pageKey(h, false)
        }
        return nil
}

// diffOf returns the diff of e, creating it on first use.
func (a *App) diffOf(e *entry) *fileDiff {
        if e.diff == nil {
                e.diff = newFileDiff(a.cfg, e.paneNames(), [2][]string{e.Old, e.New})
                e.diff.markPath = e.Path
        }
        return e.diff
}

// viewOf returns the diff view of e, creating it on first use.
func (a *App) viewOf(e *entry) *Model {
        if e.view == nil {
                e.view = newModel(a.diffOf(e))
                e.view.canBack = true
                e.view.commitNav = a.inLog()
        }
        return e.view
}

func (a *App) openFile() {
        if a.cursor >= len(a.files) {
                return
        }
        if a.cfg.Settings.Flow && !a.inLog() {
                a.showFlow(a.cursor, nil)
                return
        }
        e := a.files[a.cursor]
        if e.Binary {
                a.msg = e.Path + " is a binary file"
                return
        }
        a.viewOf(e).Update(tea.WindowSizeMsg{Width: a.width, Height: a.height})
        a.open = e
}

// showFlow shows the flow view on file, where its diff view from leaves
// it, or on its bar for from nil (see Flow.showFrom).
func (a *App) showFlow(file int, from *Model) {
        if a.flow == nil {
                a.flow = flowOfApp(a)
        }
        a.open = a.files[file]
        a.flow.width, a.flow.height = a.width, a.height
        a.flow.showFrom(0, file, from)
}

// toggleFlow switches between the diff view of the file under the cursor
// and the flow view, keeping the cursor on its line and screen row.
func (a *App) toggleFlow() {
        st := &a.cfg.Settings
        if !st.Flow {
                st.Flow = true
                switch {
                case len(a.files) == 0:
                case a.open == nil:
                        a.showFlow(a.cursor, nil)
                default:
                        a.showFlow(a.cursor, a.open.view)
                }
                return
        }
        st.Flow = false
        if a.open == nil {
                return
        }
        _, file, line := a.flow.position()
        screenRow := a.flow.cursor - a.flow.top
        a.open = nil
        if file < 0 {
                return
        }
        a.cursor = file
        a.scrollToCursor()
        if a.files[file].Binary {
                return
        }
        a.openFile()
        a.open.view.takeOver(a.flow, line, screenRow)
}

// gotoMark opens the file of mark letter, when it is in the diff, with the
// cursor on its line at screen row screenRow.
func (a *App) gotoMark(letter string, screenRow int) {
        mk, ok := a.cfg.Mark(letter)
        if !ok {
                a.setMsg("no mark " + letter)
                return
        }
        i := a.fileByPath(mk.Path)
        if i < 0 {
                a.setMsg(notInDiff(letter, mk))
                return
        }
        a.openIndex(i)
        if a.flowShown() {
                a.flow.gotoMark(letter)
                return
        }
        if at, ok := a.open.view.markSpot(letter, mk); ok {
                a.open.view.show(at, screenRow)
        }
}

// jump opens the file of j's end of moved code with the cursor on its line.
func (a *App) jump(j jumpMsg) {
        i := a.fileByGroup(j.end.file)
        if i < 0 {
                return
        }
        a.openIndex(i)
        a.open.view.show(a.open.diff.spotOf(j.end.side, j.end.line, a.open.view.shape()), j.screenRow)
        a.open.view.msg = j.msg
}

// switchFile opens the next (dir > 0) or previous file that can be shown.
func (a *App) switchFile(dir int) {
        for i := a.cursor + dir; i >= 0 && i < len(a.files); i += dir {
                if !a.files[i].Binary {
                        a.openIndex(i)
                        return
                }
        }
        a.setMsg(ends(dir > 0, "last file", "first file"))
}

// scrollPreview scrolls the preview of the file under the cursor by delta
// lines: up to its first line, down until its last one is the last shown.
func (a *App) scrollPreview(delta int) {
        e := a.files[a.cursor]
        if e.Binary {
                return
        }
        screen, at := a.diffOf(e).screenLines(a.previewLayout(e).shape)
        from := a.previewStart(e, screen, at)
        if delta > 0 {
                from = min(from+delta, max(len(screen)-(a.boxHeight()-2), from))
        } else {
                from = max(from+delta, 0)
        }
        a.previewFor, a.previewTop = e, from
}

// previewLayout returns the layout of the preview of e: as wide as the
// box's inside, scrolled like the file's diff view.
func (a *App) previewLayout(e *entry) layout {
        lo := layout{width: max(a.width-4, 0)}
        lo.shape = newShape(a.cfg, lo.width)
        if e.view != nil {
                lo.xoff, lo.active = e.view.xoff, e.view.active
        }
        return lo
}

// previewStart returns the first of the screen lines of e's diff, indexed by
// at, that its preview shows: where pgup and pgdn scrolled it, else the
// context of the first change.
func (a *App) previewStart(e *entry, screen []sline, at map[sline]int) int {
        if a.previewFor == e {
                return max(min(a.previewTop, len(screen)-1), 0)
        }
        d := a.diffOf(e)
        for i := range screen {
                if d.blockStart(screen, i) {
                        // The rows of context before the block, else its first line.
                        return min(at[sline{max(screen[i].v-a.cfg.Settings.Context, 0), 0}], i)
                }
        }
        return 0
}

// preview renders the diff of e in a box height lines high, starting with
// the context of the first change, or where pgup and pgdn scrolled it.
func (a *App) preview(e *entry, height int) string {
        t := a.cfg.Theme
        title := lipgloss.NewStyle().Bold(true).Render(" " + e.name() + " ")
        if e.Binary {
                return box(t, a.width, height, title, []string{t.Hint.Render("binary file")})
        }
        d := a.diffOf(e)
        if len(d.view) == 0 { // empty on both sides
                return box(t, a.width, height, title, nil)
        }
        lo := a.previewLayout(e)
        screen, at := d.screenLines(lo.shape)
        from := a.previewStart(e, screen, at)
        var lines []string
        for i := from; i < len(screen) && len(lines) < height-2; i++ {
                lines = append(lines, d.render(screen[i], false, lo))
        }
        return box(t, a.width, height, title, lines)
}

func (a *App) View() tea.View { return tea.NewView(a.frame()) }

func (a *App) frame() string {
        if a.flowShown() {
                return a.flow.frame()
        }
        if a.open != nil {
                return a.open.view.frame()
        }
        if a.width == 0 {
                return ""
        }

        t := a.cfg.Theme
        var added, removed int
        addW, remW, lblW := 1, 1, 0
        for _, e := range a.files {
                added, removed = added+e.added, removed+e.removed
                addW = max(addW, len(fmt.Sprint(e.added)))
                remW = max(remW, len(fmt.Sprint(e.removed)))
                lblW = max(lblW, ansi.StringWidth(e.label(t)))
        }

        // Styles are rendered side by side: lipgloss underlining breaks nested
        // escape sequences.
        bold := lipgloss.NewStyle().Bold(true)
        head := " " + bold.Render(fmt.Sprintf("%d files changed", len(a.files)))
        if c := a.commit; c != nil {
                head = " " + t.CommitHash.Render(c.Short) + " " + bold.Render(c.Subject)
        }
        head += "  " + t.Plus.Render(fmt.Sprintf("+%d", added)) + " " + t.Minus.Render(fmt.Sprintf("-%d", removed))
        if a.gitDiff != nil {
                cmd := diffCommand(a.diffArgs)
                if a.cfg.Settings.Untracked {
                        cmd += " + untracked"
                }
                head += "  " + t.Hint.Render(cmd)
        }

        item := func(i int, st func(lipgloss.Style) lipgloss.Style) string {
                e := a.files[i]
                cnt := counts(t, st, e.added, e.removed, addW, remW)
                if e.Binary {
                        cnt = st(t.Filler).Render(fmt.Sprintf("%-*s", addW+remW+3, "binary"))
                }
                lbl := st(lipgloss.NewStyle()).Render(" " + pad(e.label(t), lblW, lipgloss.NewStyle()) + " ")
                return lbl + " " + cnt + "  " + st(lipgloss.NewStyle()).Render(e.name())
        }
        k := barKeys{enter: "open", commits: a.inLog(), flow: true}
        if a.inLog() {
                k.back = "to_commits"
        }
        hints := statusKeys(a.cfg, k)
        return a.render(t, head, item, func(h int) string { return a.preview(a.files[a.cursor], h) }, pageStatus(a.cfg, a.msg, hints))
}

package ui

import (
        "cmp"
        "fmt"
        "io/fs"
        "path"
        "slices"
        "strings"

        tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
        "charm.land/lipgloss/v2"
        "github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"

        "jdiff/internal/config"
)

// Doc shows the pages of jdiff's documentation, markdown rendered for the
// terminal: jdiff --readme. A cursor line moves through a page; enter follows
// the link it is on, the first unless h l or tab chose another, to another
// page or heading, backspace goes back. Only links that lead to a file of
// docs, and a heading there, or to the web are links. A file that is no
// markdown, reached by a link, shows as it is.
type Doc struct {
        cfg      *config.Config
        docs     fs.FS
        order    []string                  // the pages in docs, in the order of n and p
        page     string                    // the file of docs shown
        anchors  map[string]map[string]int // the headings of other pages, see anchorsOf
        rendered docPage
        forWidth int // the width rendered is for, 0 before the first
        top      int // the first line shown
        cursor   int // the line of the cursor
        link     int // the selected link, on the cursor's line; -1 for none
        back     []docPlace
        msg      string
        width    int
        height   int
}

// docPlace is where a link was followed from, and the link.
type docPlace struct {
        page              string
        top, cursor, link int
}

// NewDoc returns the documentation in docs, whose pages are order, on page
// order[page].
func NewDoc(cfg *config.Config, docs fs.FS, order []string, page int) *Doc {
        return &Doc{cfg: cfg, docs: docs, order: order, page: order[page], anchors: map[string]map[string]int{}, link: -1}
}

func (d *Doc) Init() tea.Cmd { return nil }

func (d *Doc) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
        switch msg := msg.(type) {
        case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
                d.width, d.height = msg.Width, msg.Height
                d.render()
        case tea.KeyPressMsg:
                return d, d.key(msg.String())
        case findMsg:
                d.findText(int(msg))
        }
        return d, nil
}

// textWidth is the width pages are rendered for: the screen but a column
// on each side, the left one for the cursor.
func (d *Doc) textWidth() int { return max(d.width-2, 1) }

// bodyHeight is the number of lines shown, between the title and the status
// bar.
func (d *Doc) bodyHeight() int { return max(d.height-2, 1) }

// render renders the page for the width of the screen, unless it is already.
func (d *Doc) render() {
        if d.forWidth == d.textWidth() {
                return
        }
        src, err := fs.ReadFile(d.docs, d.page)
        if err != nil {
                d.msg = err.Error()
        }
        d.forWidth = d.textWidth()
        if path.Ext(d.page) == ".md" {
                d.rendered = renderMarkdown(src, d.forWidth)
        } else {
                d.rendered = renderText(src, d.forWidth)
        }
        for i, l := range d.rendered.links {
                _, _, ok := d.resolve(l.target)
                d.rendered.links[i].broken = !ok
        }
        d.scrollTo(d.top)
        d.moveTo(d.cursor)
}

// show shows page from line top, the cursor on line cursor.
func (d *Doc) show(page string, top, cursor int) {
        d.page, d.forWidth = page, 0
        d.render()
        d.scrollTo(top)
        d.moveTo(cursor)
}

// scrollTo scrolls to line top, kept where lines fill the screen.
func (d *Doc) scrollTo(top int) {
        d.top = max(min(top, len(d.rendered.lines)-d.bodyHeight()), 0)
}

// moveTo moves the cursor to line i, kept in the page, and scrolls it into
// view; the first link on the line that resolves is selected.
func (d *Doc) moveTo(i int) {
        n := len(d.rendered.lines)
        d.cursor = max(min(i, n-1), 0)
        d.top = scrollWindow(d.cursor, d.top, d.bodyHeight(), n)
        d.link = -1
        if d.cursor < n {
                for _, s := range d.rendered.lines[d.cursor].segs {
                        if d.live(s.link) {
                                d.link = s.link
                                break
                        }
                }
        }
}

func (d *Doc) key(k string) tea.Cmd {
        d.msg = ""
        h := keyFile.key(docView, k)
        body := d.bodyHeight()
        if delta, paging, ok := motion(h, max(body-1, 1), len(d.rendered.lines)); ok {
                if paging {
                        d.scrollTo(d.top + delta)
                }
                d.moveTo(d.cursor + delta)
                return nil
        }
        switch h.action {
        case "quit":
                return tea.Quit
        case "half_page":
                delta := direction(h.i == 1) * (body / 2)
                d.scrollTo(d.top + delta)
                d.moveTo(d.cursor + delta)
        case "link":
                d.nextLink(direction(h.i == 0))
        case "line_link":
                d.lineLink(direction(h.i == 1))
        case "follow":
                d.follow()
        case "back":
                if len(d.back) == 0 {
                        d.msg = "no link followed to go back from"
                        break
                }
                p := d.back[len(d.back)-1]
                d.back = d.back[:len(d.back)-1]
                d.show(p.page, p.top, p.cursor)
                d.link = p.link
        case "next_page":
                i := slices.Index(d.order, d.page)
                next := i + direction(h.i == 0)
                switch {
                case i < 0:
                        d.msg = d.page + " is no page of " + keyFile.keyText("{next_page}") + ": " + keyFile.keyText("{back}") + " goes back"
                case next < 0 || next >= len(d.order):
                        d.msg = ends(h.i == 0, "last page", "first page")
                default:
                        d.show(d.order[next], 0, 0)
                }
        case "search":
                return askSearch
        case "match":
                d.findText(direction(h.i == 0))
        }
        return nil
}

// live reports whether link i of the page is one: it resolves.
func (d *Doc) live(i int) bool { return i >= 0 && !d.rendered.links[i].broken }

// nextLink moves the cursor to the next link in direction dir and selects
// it: the one after the selected link, also on the same line, or the first
// below or last above the cursor.
func (d *Doc) nextLink(dir int) {
        links := d.rendered.links
        next := -1
        for i := range links {
                j := i
                if dir < 0 {
                        j = len(links) - 1 - i
                }
                after := d.link >= 0 && (j-d.link)*dir > 0 || d.link < 0 && (links[j].line-d.cursor)*dir > 0
                if after && d.live(j) {
                        next = j
                        break
                }
        }
        if next < 0 {
                d.msg = ends(dir > 0, "no link further down", "no link further up")
                return
        }
        d.moveTo(links[next].line)
        d.link = next
}

// lineLink selects the next link on the cursor's line in direction dir,
// right or left.
func (d *Doc) lineLink(dir int) {
        var on []int
        for _, s := range d.rendered.lines[min(d.cursor, len(d.rendered.lines)-1)].segs {
                if d.live(s.link) && !slices.Contains(on, s.link) {
                        on = append(on, s.link)
                }
        }
        i := slices.Index(on, d.link) + dir
        switch {
        case len(on) == 0:
                d.msg = "no link on this line"
        case i < 0 || i >= len(on):
                d.msg = ends(dir > 0, "no link further right", "no link further left")
        default:
                d.link = on[i]
        }
}

// follow follows the selected link to its page and heading, which shows at
// the top; for a web address the box of frame shows it already.
func (d *Doc) follow() {
        if d.link < 0 {
                d.msg = "no link on this line: " + keyFile.keyText("{link}") + " go to the next, previous"
                return
        }
        target := d.rendered.links[d.link].target
        page, anchor, _ := d.resolve(target)
        if page == "" {
                return
        }
        d.back = append(d.back, docPlace{d.page, d.top, d.cursor, d.link})
        d.show(page, 0, 0)
        if line := d.rendered.anchors[anchor]; anchor != "" {
                d.show(page, line, line)
        }
}

// resolve returns the file of docs and the heading that target, a link of
// the page shown, goes to, and whether both are there; for a web address
// the file is "".
func (d *Doc) resolve(target string) (page, anchor string, ok bool) {
        file, anchor, _ := strings.Cut(target, "#")
        if strings.Contains(file, ":") { // https:, mailto:
                return "", "", true
        }
        page = d.page
        if file != "" {
                page = path.Join(path.Dir(d.page), file)
                if _, err := fs.Stat(d.docs, page); err != nil {
                        return page, anchor, false
                }
        }
        if anchor == "" {
                return page, "", true
        }
        _, ok = d.anchorsOf(page)[anchor]
        return page, anchor, ok
}

// anchorsOf returns the headings of page by the names links give them, with
// their lines for the width of the page shown when it is that page.
func (d *Doc) anchorsOf(page string) map[string]int {
        if page == d.page {
                return d.rendered.anchors
        }
        a, ok := d.anchors[page]
        if !ok {
                if src, err := fs.ReadFile(d.docs, page); err == nil && path.Ext(page) == ".md" {
                        a = renderMarkdown(src, 80).anchors
                }
                d.anchors[page] = a
        }
        return a
}

// findText moves the cursor to the next line, in direction dir, that holds
// what config.Search searched for, past the cursor's own line.
func (d *Doc) findText(dir int) {
        q := d.cfg.Search.Query
        m := matcherOf(q)
        if m == nil {
                d.msg = nothingSearched
                return
        }
        var hits []int
        for i, l := range d.rendered.lines {
                if len(m.spans(l.plain())) > 0 {
                        hits = append(hits, i)
                }
        }
        if len(hits) == 0 {
                d.msg = "no match for " + q.Text + " in this page"
                return
        }
        i, ok := nextHit(len(hits), dir, func(i int) int { return cmp.Compare(hits[i], d.cursor) })
        if !ok {
                d.msg = noFurther(q, dir, "")
                return
        }
        d.moveTo(hits[i])
        d.msg = searchMsg(q, i, len(hits))
}

func (d *Doc) View() tea.View { return tea.NewView(d.frame()) }

func (d *Doc) frame() string {
        if d.width == 0 {
                return ""
        }
        t := d.cfg.Theme
        lines := d.rendered.lines
        var b strings.Builder
        var where []string
        if i := slices.Index(d.order, d.page); i >= 0 {
                where = append(where, fmt.Sprintf("page %d/%d", i+1, len(d.order)))
        }
        if n := len(lines); n > d.bodyHeight() {
                where = append(where, fmt.Sprintf("%d%%", 100*min(d.top+d.bodyHeight(), n)/n))
        }
        b.WriteString(statusLine(t, d.width, t.Title.Render(" "+printable(d.page)), strings.Join(where, " · ")+" ") + "\n")
        m := matcherOf(d.cfg.Search.Query)
        for i := d.top; i < d.top+d.bodyHeight(); i++ {
                if i < len(lines) {
                        margin := " "
                        if i == d.cursor {
                                margin = t.Cursor.Render(margin)
                        }
                        b.WriteString(margin + d.drawLine(lines[i], m))
                }
                b.WriteString("\n")
        }
        keys := statusKeys(d.cfg, barKeys{enter: "follow", back: "back", text: true})
        b.WriteString(statusLine(t, d.width, pageStatus(d.cfg, d.msg, keys), ""))
        return d.withWebAddress(b.String())
}

// withWebAddress returns frame with a box of the web address of the selected
// link below its line, or above it near the bottom, while the cursor is on
// it; links into the documentation need none.
func (d *Doc) withWebAddress(frame string) string {
        if d.link < 0 {
                return frame
        }
        target := d.rendered.links[d.link].target
        if page, _, _ := d.resolve(target); page != "" {
                return frame
        }
        row := 1 + d.cursor - d.top // in frame, below the title
        y := row + 1
        if y+minBoxHeight > 1+d.bodyHeight() {
                y = row - minBoxHeight
        }
        if y < 1 {
                return frame // no room
        }
        x := 1 // the cursor's column, then the line up to the link
        for _, s := range d.rendered.lines[d.cursor].segs {
                if s.link == d.link {
                        break
                }
                x += ansi.StringWidth(s.text)
        }
        address := printable(target)
        w := min(ansi.StringWidth(address)+4, d.width)
        return overlayAt(frame, box(d.cfg.Theme, w, minBoxHeight, "", []string{address}), max(min(x, d.width-w), 0), y, w, d.width)
}

// drawLine draws l in the styles of its pieces, the selected link in the
// cursor style and what m finds in the search style.
func (d *Doc) drawLine(l docLine, m *matcher) string {
        var found []bool
        if m != nil {
                plain := l.plain()
                found = make([]bool, len(plain))
                for _, s := range m.spans(plain) {
                        for j := s.Start; j < s.End; j++ {
                                found[j] = true
                        }
                }
        }
        var b strings.Builder
        off := 0
        for _, s := range l.segs {
                st := d.style(s.style)
                switch {
                case s.link >= 0 && s.link == d.link:
                        st = d.cfg.Theme.Cursor
                case s.link >= 0 && !d.live(s.link):
                        st = d.style(docPlain) // leads nowhere
                }
                // Runs of the piece in or out of a match.
                for start := 0; start < len(s.text); {
                        end := start + 1
                        in := found != nil && found[off+start]
                        for end < len(s.text) && (found != nil && found[off+end]) == in {
                                end++
                        }
                        if in {
                                b.WriteString(d.cfg.Theme.Search.Render(s.text[start:end]))
                        } else {
                                b.WriteString(st.Render(s.text[start:end]))
                        }
                        start = end
                }
                off += len(s.text)
        }
        return b.String()
}

// style returns the style of the theme for a piece of a page.
func (d *Doc) style(s docStyle) lipgloss.Style {
        t := d.cfg.Theme
        switch s {
        case docBold:
                return lipgloss.NewStyle().Bold(true)
        case docItalic:
                return lipgloss.NewStyle().Italic(true)
        case docCode:
                return t.DocCode
        case docHeading:
                return t.DocHeading
        case docLink:
                return t.DocLink
        case docDecoration:
                return t.Decoration
        case docOverflow:
                return t.Overflow
        }
        return lipgloss.NewStyle()
}

package ui

import (
        "fmt"
        "slices"
        "strings"

        "github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"

        "jdiff/internal/config"
        "jdiff/internal/diff"
        "jdiff/internal/settings"
        "jdiff/internal/theme"
)

type side int

const (
        left side = iota
        right
)

func (s side) other() side { return 1 - s }

// lineOf returns the line of side s shown in r, or -1.
func lineOf(r diff.Row, s side) int {
        if s == left {
                return r.Left
        }
        return r.Right
}

// emphOf returns the emphasized parts of the line of side s in r.
func emphOf(r diff.Row, s side) []diff.Span {
        if s == left {
                return r.LeftEmph
        }
        return r.RightEmph
}

// anchorLine returns the line of side s that an anchors.
func anchorLine(an diff.Anchor, s side) int {
        if s == left {
                return an.Left
        }
        return an.Right
}

// mark is a line chosen as the first half of a new anchor.
type mark struct {
        side side
        line int
}

// vrow is a displayed row: the diff row row, or when hidden > 0 a fold
// standing for the unchanged rows [row, row+hidden). block is the index of
// the foldable block the row belongs to, or -1.
type vrow struct{ row, hidden, block int }

// sline is a screen line of a diff: line sub of displayed row v. Side by
// side a displayed row is one line, or with wrapping as many as its longer
// line needs; in the normal layout a changed row has the lines of its
// removed line, then of its added line, each as many as it needs, which
// screenLines puts into the old and the new half of their block.
type sline struct{ v, sub int }

// block is a foldable run of unchanged rows [start, end).
type block struct{ start, end int }

// spot is a cursor in a diff: on line sub of displayed row v, in the pane of
// side side.
type spot struct {
        v, sub int
        side   side
}

// shape is what the screen lines of a diff depend on: the normal layout
// when unified, the width of the view when wrap is not 0, at which long
// lines are wrapped instead of cut, and with lineEnd the column of the
// symbol at the end of lines (line_end, one column).
type shape struct {
        unified bool
        wrap    int
        lineEnd bool
        cr      bool // the \r of all \r\n line ends is drawn, see crShown
}

// layout is how a view draws a diff: width columns wide, scrolled by xoff
// columns unless lines are wrapped, with the cursor in the pane of side
// active, in shape.
type layout struct {
        width, xoff int
        active      side
        shape
}

// fileDiff is the diff of one file as the user anchored and folded it. The
// single file view, the flow view and the preview all show the same
// fileDiff, each with its own cursor.
type fileDiff struct {
        cfg   *config.Config
        names [2]string
        lines [2][]string

        anchors []diff.Anchor // sorted by Left, never crossing
        rows    []diff.Row
        rowOf   [2][]int // line index -> row index, per side

        blocks   []block      // foldable blocks of unchanged rows
        foldOpen map[int]bool // open state by the block's first left line
        view     []vrow       // displayed rows
        viewOf   []int        // row index -> view index
        changes  int          // counts the rebuilds of view

        pending *mark        // first half of an anchor being created
        replace *diff.Anchor // anchor waiting for confirmation to replace conflicts

        // The last reload: it replaced the lines of side reloadSide, moved maps
        // their old indices to the new ones, -1 for lines gone. reloads counts
        // the reloads.
        reloads    int
        reloadSide side
        moved      []int

        widths [2][]int // the columns each line takes when drawn, per side
        // highlighted is the line drawn last, per side, see content.
        highlighted [2]highlightCache

        markPath   string     // the file's path in marks
        aligns     int        // counts the alignments
        group      *moveGroup // the files searched for moved code with this one
        groupIndex int        // the index of this file in group
}

// highlightKey is what the highlight of a line depends on: the line, its row
// in the alignment, the cursor on it, whether it moved, the text searched
// for, the settings and the theme.
type highlightKey struct {
        line, aligns  int
        cursor, moved bool
        search        config.Query
        settings      settings.Settings
        theme         *theme.Theme
}

type highlightCache struct {
        key highlightKey
        out string
        ok  bool
}

func newFileDiff(cfg *config.Config, names [2]string, lines [2][]string) *fileDiff {
        d := &fileDiff{cfg: cfg, names: names, lines: lines, foldOpen: map[int]bool{}}
        d.measure(left)
        d.measure(right)
        d.align()
        return d
}

// measure computes the widths of the lines of side s, without the \r of a
// \r\n line end: see crShown.
func (d *fileDiff) measure(s side) {
        d.widths[s] = make([]int, len(d.lines[s]))
        for i, l := range d.lines[s] {
                d.widths[s][i] = ansi.StringWidth(printable(strings.TrimSuffix(l, "\r")))
        }
}

// crShown reports whether line of side s ends in a \r that is drawn in sh:
// where it changed, or on all lines with sh.cr.
func (d *fileDiff) crShown(s side, line int, sh shape) bool {
        text := d.lines[s][line]
        if !strings.HasSuffix(text, "\r") {
                return false
        }
        if sh.cr {
                return true
        }
        for _, sp := range emphOf(d.rows[d.rowOf[s][line]], s) {
                if sp.End == len(text) {
                        return true
                }
        }
        return false
}

// reload replaces the lines of side s by lines, the file's new content on
// disk. Lines that are unchanged keep their anchors and folds; anchors on
// lines that are gone are dropped and counted. A pending mark or replace
// prompt is cancelled. Views follow their cursor line through moved, so
// they must catch up before the next reload.
func (d *fileDiff) reload(s side, lines []string) (dropped int) {
        moved := lineMap(d.lines[s], lines)
        var anchors []diff.Anchor
        for _, an := range d.anchors {
                n := moved[anchorLine(an, s)]
                if n < 0 {
                        dropped++
                        continue
                }
                if s == left {
                        an.Left = n
                } else {
                        an.Right = n
                }
                anchors = append(anchors, an)
        }
        d.anchors = anchors
        if s == left {
                open := map[int]bool{}
                for line, o := range d.foldOpen {
                        if n := moved[line]; n >= 0 {
                                open[n] = o
                        }
                }
                d.foldOpen = open
        }
        d.pending, d.replace = nil, nil
        d.lines[s] = lines
        d.measure(s)
        d.reloads, d.reloadSide, d.moved = d.reloads+1, s, moved
        d.align()
        return dropped
}

// follow returns the displayed row that now shows a line of r, a row before
// the diff changed: its left line, else its right one, through the last
// reload when reloaded is set. It returns -1 when neither line is left.
func (d *fileDiff) follow(r diff.Row, reloaded bool) int {
        if i := d.rowNow(r, reloaded); i >= 0 {
                return d.viewOf[i]
        }
        return -1
}

// rowAfterReload returns the index of the row that now holds a line of r, a
// row before the last reload, as follow does, or -1.
func (d *fileDiff) rowAfterReload(r diff.Row) int { return d.rowNow(r, true) }

// rowNow returns the index of the row that now holds the left line of r,
// else its right one, through the last reload when reloaded is set, or -1.
func (d *fileDiff) rowNow(r diff.Row, reloaded bool) int {
        for _, s := range []side{left, right} {
                line := lineOf(r, s)
                if line >= 0 && reloaded && s == d.reloadSide {
                        line = d.moved[line]
                }
                if line >= 0 {
                        return d.rowOf[s][line]
                }
        }
        return -1
}

// lineMap maps the indices of old to those of the same lines in new, -1
// for lines that are not in new. It keeps the order of the lines.
func lineMap(old, new []string) []int {
        moved := make([]int, len(old))
        for i := range moved {
                moved[i] = -1
        }
        for _, r := range diff.Align(old, new, nil, diff.Options{}) {
                if r.Kind == diff.Equal {
                        moved[r.Left] = r.Right
                }
        }
        return moved
}

// act runs the anchor and fold action (anchor, cancel_mark, fold or
// fold_all) at the cursor at, in the screen lines of sh. It returns the cursor
// afterwards and the message to show, "" for none.
func (d *fileDiff) act(action string, at spot, sh shape) (spot, string) {
        if d.replace != nil {
                an := *d.replace
                d.replace = nil
                switch action {
                case "anchor":
                        for _, c := range diff.Conflicts(d.anchors, an) {
                                d.removeAnchor(c)
                        }
                        return d.addAnchor(an, at.side, sh)
                case "cancel_mark":
                        return at, "replace cancelled"
                }
        }
        switch action {
        case "cancel_mark":
                if d.pending != nil {
                        d.pending = nil
                        return at, "mark cancelled"
                }
        case "anchor":
                return d.enter(at, sh)
        case "fold":
                return d.toggleNearestBlock(at)
        case "fold_all":
                return d.toggleAllBlocks(at)
        }
        return at, ""
}

// markPrompt returns what to do with the pending mark, "" without one: the
// status lines show it until another message, while the mark waits.
func (d *fileDiff) markPrompt() string {
        if d.pending == nil {
                return ""
        }
        return fmt.Sprintf("marked %s%d, pick the matching line (esc cancels)", sideName(d.pending.side), d.pending.line+1)
}

// enter implements the anchor workflow: mark a line, then pick its partner
// in the other pane. Enter on an anchored line removes that anchor. In the
// normal layout the line under the cursor decides the side.
func (d *fileDiff) enter(at spot, sh shape) (spot, string) {
        if sh.unified {
                at.side = d.unifiedSide(at, sh)
        }
        line := d.lineAt(at.side, at.v)
        if line < 0 {
                if at.v < len(d.view) && d.view[at.v].hidden > 0 {
                        return at, "unchanged lines folded, space expands"
                }
                return at, "no line here"
        }
        if an, ok := d.anchorAt(at.side, line); ok {
                d.removeAnchor(an)
                d.align()
                return d.spotOf(at.side, line, sh), "removed anchor " + anchorName(an)
        }
        if d.pending == nil || d.pending.side == at.side {
                if d.pending != nil && d.pending.line == line {
                        d.pending = nil
                        return at, "mark cancelled"
                }
                d.pending = &mark{at.side, line}
                at.side = at.side.other()
                return at, d.markPrompt()
        }

        an := diff.Anchor{Left: d.pending.line, Right: line}
        if at.side == left {
                an = diff.Anchor{Left: line, Right: d.pending.line}
        }
        if conflicts := diff.Conflicts(d.anchors, an); len(conflicts) > 0 {
                names := make([]string, len(conflicts))
                for i, c := range conflicts {
