                        names[i] = anchorName(c)
                }
                d.replace = &an
                return at, fmt.Sprintf("%s crosses %s: enter replaces, esc cancels",
                        anchorName(an), strings.Join(names, ", "))
        }
        return d.addAnchor(an, at.side, sh)
}

// addAnchor adds an, picked last on side s, and returns the cursor on its
// row in the screen lines of sh: the line picked last keeps its place, the
// rest aligns around it.
func (d *fileDiff) addAnchor(an diff.Anchor, s side, sh shape) (spot, string) {
        i, _ := slices.BinarySearchFunc(d.anchors, an, func(x, y diff.Anchor) int {
                return x.Left - y.Left
        })
        d.anchors = slices.Insert(d.anchors, i, an)
        d.pending = nil
        d.align()
        return d.spotOf(s, anchorLine(an, s), sh), "anchored " + anchorName(an)
}

// unifiedSide returns the side of the line under the cursor at in the normal
// layout with the screen lines of sh. An unchanged line belongs to both: it
// takes the side opposite a pending mark, else the old side.
func (d *fileDiff) unifiedSide(at spot, sh shape) side {
        if at.v >= len(d.view) || d.view[at.v].hidden > 0 {
                return at.side
        }
        switch r := d.rows[d.view[at.v].row]; {
        case r.Kind == diff.Delete:
                return left
        case r.Kind == diff.Insert:
                return right
        case r.Kind == diff.Change:
                s, _ := d.unifiedPiece(at.v, at.sub, sh)
                return s
        case d.pending != nil:
                return d.pending.side.other()
        }
        return left
}

func (d *fileDiff) removeAnchor(an diff.Anchor) {
        d.anchors = slices.DeleteFunc(d.anchors, func(x diff.Anchor) bool { return x == an })
}

func (d *fileDiff) anchorAt(s side, line int) (diff.Anchor, bool) {
        for _, an := range d.anchors {
                if anchorLine(an, s) == line {
                        return an, true
                }
        }
        return diff.Anchor{}, false
}

// align computes the rows from the lines and anchors, then the displayed
// rows.
func (d *fileDiff) align() {
        d.rows = diff.Align(d.lines[left], d.lines[right], d.anchors,
                diff.Options{SplitIndent: d.cfg.Settings.OnlyChangedWhitespaces})
        for s := range d.rowOf {
                d.rowOf[s] = make([]int, len(d.lines[s]))
        }
        for i, r := range d.rows {
                if r.Left >= 0 {
                        d.rowOf[left][r.Left] = i
                }
                if r.Right >= 0 {
                        d.rowOf[right][r.Right] = i
                }
        }
        d.aligns++
        d.buildView()
}

// buildView computes the displayed rows. Unchanged rows further than the
// context from any change, anchor or pending mark form foldable blocks,
// shown as one fold row while closed; a single such row is never a block.
// The view is built anew, so views holding the old one can still read it.
func (d *fileDiff) buildView() {
        keep := make([]bool, len(d.rows))
        ctx := max(d.cfg.Settings.Context, 0)
        for i, r := range d.rows {
                if r.Kind != diff.Equal || r.Anchored || d.isPending(r) {
                        for j := max(i-ctx, 0); j <= min(i+ctx, len(d.rows)-1); j++ {
                                keep[j] = true
                        }
                }
        }

        d.blocks = nil
        d.view = nil
        d.viewOf = make([]int, len(d.rows))
        for i := 0; i < len(d.rows); {
                j := i + 1
                if !keep[i] {
                        for j < len(d.rows) && !keep[j] {
                                j++
                        }
                }
                if j-i < 2 {
                        d.viewOf[i] = len(d.view)
                        d.view = append(d.view, vrow{row: i, block: -1})
                        i = j
                        continue
                }
                b := len(d.blocks)
                d.blocks = append(d.blocks, block{i, j})
                open := d.blockOpen(d.blocks[b])
                for k := i; k < j; k++ {
                        d.viewOf[k] = len(d.view)
                        if open {
                                d.view = append(d.view, vrow{row: k, block: b})
                        }
                }
                if !open {
                        d.view = append(d.view, vrow{row: i, hidden: j - i, block: b})
                }
                i = j
        }
        d.changes++
}

// newShape returns the shape of the screen lines of a view width columns
// wide, as the settings of cfg ask for.
func newShape(cfg *config.Config, width int) shape {
        sh := shape{unified: !cfg.Settings.SideBySide}
        if cfg.Settings.Wrap {
                sh.wrap, sh.lineEnd, sh.cr = width, cfg.Settings.ShowWhitespaces, cfg.Settings.ShowCR
        }
        return sh
}

// contentWidth returns the columns left for the text of a line in a view
// width columns wide, in the normal layout when unified, else side by side.
func (d *fileDiff) contentWidth(width int, unified bool) int {
        nw := d.numWidth()
        if unified {
                return max(width-2*nw-4, 0)
        }
        return max((width-1)/2-nw-3, 0)
}

// pieces returns the number of screen lines line of side s takes in sh: 1,
// or the pieces it is wrapped into. A missing line takes 1.
func (d *fileDiff) pieces(s side, line int, sh shape) int {
        cw := d.contentWidth(sh.wrap, sh.unified)
        if sh.wrap == 0 || line < 0 || cw == 0 {
                return 1
        }
        w := d.widths[s][line]
        if sh.lineEnd {
                w++
        }
        if d.crShown(s, line, sh) {
                w++
        }
        return max((w+cw-1)/cw, 1)
}

// rowLines returns the number of screen lines of displayed row v in sh.
func (d *fileDiff) rowLines(v int, sh shape) int {
        if d.view[v].hidden > 0 {
                return 1
        }
        r := d.rows[d.view[v].row]
        switch {
        case !sh.unified:
                return max(d.pieces(left, r.Left, sh), d.pieces(right, r.Right, sh))
        case r.Kind == diff.Change:
                return d.pieces(left, r.Left, sh) + d.pieces(right, r.Right, sh)
        }
        s, _ := d.unifiedPiece(v, 0, sh)
        return d.pieces(s, lineOf(r, s), sh)
}

// unifiedPiece returns the side whose line screen line sub of displayed row
// v shows in the normal layout with sh, and which piece of it.
func (d *fileDiff) unifiedPiece(v, sub int, sh shape) (side, int) {
        if d.view[v].hidden > 0 {
                return right, 0
        }
        switch r := d.rows[d.view[v].row]; r.Kind {
        case diff.Delete:
                return left, sub
        case diff.Change:
                if n := d.pieces(left, r.Left, sh); sub >= n {
                        return right, sub - n
                }
                return left, sub
        }
        return right, sub
}

// firstSub returns the screen line of displayed row v that starts the line
// of side s in sh. Side by side without wrapping it is the one of the normal
// layout: the view clamps it to the row's only line, and it keeps the side
// for switching back.
func (d *fileDiff) firstSub(v int, s side, sh shape) int {
        if !sh.unified && sh.wrap > 0 {
                return 0
        }
        r := d.rows[d.view[v].row]
        if d.view[v].hidden > 0 || r.Kind != diff.Change || s == left {
                return 0
        }
        return d.pieces(left, r.Left, sh)
}

// convertSub returns the screen line of displayed row v in sh that shows
// what screen line sub in from shows: the same piece of the same side's
// line, as far as it has one. Side by side with wrapping, active is the side
// of the cursor. Without wrapping in either shape, sub is kept as it is, as
// it was before lines could wrap.
func (d *fileDiff) convertSub(v, sub int, from, sh shape, active side) int {
        if from.wrap == 0 && sh.wrap == 0 || d.view[v].hidden > 0 {
                return sub
        }
        s, piece := active, sub
        switch {
        case from.unified:
                s, piece = d.unifiedPiece(v, sub, from)
        case from.wrap == 0:
                s, piece = d.unifiedPiece(v, sub, shape{unified: true})
        }
        if !sh.unified && sh.wrap == 0 {
                return d.firstSub(v, s, sh)
        }
        r := d.rows[d.view[v].row]
        if !sh.unified {
                return min(piece, d.rowLines(v, sh)-1)
        }
        if lineOf(r, s) < 0 || (r.Kind != diff.Change && r.Kind != diff.Equal) {
                s, _ = d.unifiedPiece(v, 0, sh) // the row shows only one side
        }
        return d.firstSub(v, s, sh) + min(piece, d.pieces(s, lineOf(r, s), sh)-1)
}

// screenLines returns the screen lines of the displayed rows in sh and the
// index of each of them. In the normal layout a block of changed rows shows
// all its old lines, then all its new ones, as git diff does; so the two
// lines of a changed row are not next to each other.
func (d *fileDiff) screenLines(sh shape) (screen []sline, at map[sline]int) {
        at = make(map[sline]int, len(d.view))
        add := func(v, sub int) {
                at[sline{v, sub}] = len(screen)
                screen = append(screen, sline{v, sub})
        }
        for v := 0; v < len(d.view); {
                if !sh.unified || !d.changed(v) {
                        for sub := range d.rowLines(v, sh) {
                                add(v, sub)
                        }
                        v++
                        continue
                }
                end := v
                for end < len(d.view) && d.changed(end) {
                        end++
                }
                for _, s := range []side{left, right} {
                        for w := v; w < end; w++ {
                                for sub := range d.rowLines(w, sh) {
                                        if of, _ := d.unifiedPiece(w, sub, sh); of == s {
                                                add(w, sub)
                                        }
                                }
                        }
                }
                v = end
        }
        return screen, at
}

// blockStart reports whether screen line i of screen starts a block of
// changed rows.
func (d *fileDiff) blockStart(screen []sline, i int) bool {
        return d.changed(screen[i].v) && (i == 0 || !d.changed(screen[i-1].v))
}

// blockOpen reports whether b is unfolded. Blocks start closed when
// collapse_unchanged is set; toggled state is kept by the block's first
// line, so it survives realigning as long as the block starts there.
func (d *fileDiff) blockOpen(b block) bool {
        if open, ok := d.foldOpen[d.rows[b.start].Left]; ok {
                return open
        }
        return !d.cfg.Settings.CollapseUnchanged
}

func (d *fileDiff) isPending(r diff.Row) bool {
        return d.pending != nil && lineOf(r, d.pending.side) == d.pending.line
}

// toggleNearestBlock folds or unfolds the block of unchanged rows under the
// cursor at, or else the one closest to it (the lower one on a tie). The
// cursor stays on its row, or on the fold containing it.
func (d *fileDiff) toggleNearestBlock(at spot) (spot, string) {
        b := d.nearestBlock(at.v)
        if b < 0 {
                return at, "no unchanged code to fold"
        }
        r := d.view[at.v].row
        blk := d.blocks[b]
        d.foldOpen[d.rows[blk.start].Left] = !d.blockOpen(blk)
        d.buildView()
        at.v = d.viewOf[r]
        return at, ""
}

// toggleAllBlocks unfolds all blocks of unchanged rows when one of them is
// folded, and folds them all otherwise. The cursor at stays on its row, or
// on the fold containing it.
func (d *fileDiff) toggleAllBlocks(at spot) (spot, string) {
        if len(d.blocks) == 0 {
                return at, "no unchanged code to fold"
        }
        open := false
        for _, b := range d.blocks {
                if !d.blockOpen(b) {
                        open = true
                        break
                }
        }
        r := d.view[at.v].row
        for _, b := range d.blocks {
                d.foldOpen[d.rows[b.start].Left] = open
        }
        d.buildView()
        at.v = d.viewOf[r]
        return at, ""
}

// nearestBlock returns the index of the block at or closest to displayed
// row v, or -1 when there is none.
func (d *fileDiff) nearestBlock(v int) int {
        for dist := 0; dist < len(d.view); dist++ {
                for _, w := range []int{v + dist, v - dist} {
                        if w >= 0 && w < len(d.view) && d.view[w].block >= 0 {
                                return d.view[w].block
                        }
                }
        }
        return -1
}

// lineAt returns the line index shown on side s of displayed row v, or -1.
func (d *fileDiff) lineAt(s side, v int) int {
        if v < 0 || v >= len(d.view) || d.view[v].hidden > 0 {
                return -1
        }
        return lineOf(d.rows[d.view[v].row], s)
}

// changed reports whether displayed row v is a changed row, not unchanged or
// a fold.
func (d *fileDiff) changed(v int) bool {
        return d.view[v].hidden == 0 && d.rows[d.view[v].row].Kind != diff.Equal
}

func sideName(s side) string {
        if s == left {
                return "L"
        }
        return "R"
}

func anchorName(an diff.Anchor) string {
        return fmt.Sprintf("L%d↔R%d", an.Left+1, an.Right+1)
}

package ui

import (
        "fmt"
        "slices"
        "strings"

        tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
        "charm.land/lipgloss/v2"
        "github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"

        "jdiff/internal/config"
        "jdiff/internal/diff"
        "jdiff/internal/gitsrc"
)

// flowKind is the kind of a row of the flow view.
type flowKind int

const (
        flowCommit flowKind = iota // a line of a commit's header
        flowFile                   // the bar starting a file
        flowNote                   // a note in place of a diff: binary file, load error
        flowDiff                   // a displayed row of a file's diff view
)

// flowRow is a row of the flow view. part is the index of its part, file
// the index of its file within the part (-1 for rows of the commit), and
// line the line of the commit header or the index into the file's
// displayed rows; sub is the line of a diff row in the normal layout.
type flowRow struct {
        kind                  flowKind
        part, file, line, sub int
}

// flowPart is one commit of the log, or all files outside the log.
type flowPart struct {
        commit *commitEntry // nil outside the log
        full   []string     // commit header lines with the whole message, the first one is the bar
        short  []string     // commit header lines with the subject only
        app    *App         // the files, nil until loaded
        err    string       // why the files could not be loaded
}

// far moves the cursor to the first or last row.
const far = 1 << 30

// Flow is the Bubble Tea model of the flow view: the diffs of all files one
// after the other, each starting with a bar, and in the log each commit
// starting with its message. Files share their diffs with the single file
// view, so anchors and folds carry over; cursor and scrolling are the
// flow's own. Commits are loaded once they come near the cursor.
type Flow struct {
        cfg   *config.Config
        parts []*flowPart
        load  func(part int) (*App, error) // loads the files of a commit, nil outside the log
        order historyOrder                 // of the commits, the parts in the log

        rows      []flowRow
        built     shape // what the rows of the diffs were built for
        cursor    int
        top       int
        xoff      int
        active    side
        marked    *fileDiff // diff holding a pending mark
        replacing *fileDiff // diff waiting for confirmation to replace anchors
        prefix    string    // "m" or "'" while waiting for the letter of a jump mark

        width, height int
        msg           string
}

func newFlow(cfg *config.Config, parts []*flowPart, load func(int) (*App, error)) *Flow {
        f := &Flow{cfg: cfg, parts: parts, load: load}
        f.build()
        return f
}

// flowOfApp returns the flow view of the files of a, outside the log.
func flowOfApp(a *App) *Flow { return newFlow(a.cfg, []*flowPart{{app: a}}, nil) }

// newCommitPart returns the part of the flow view for commit e.
func newCommitPart(e *commitEntry) *flowPart {
        return &flowPart{commit: e, full: commitHeader(e.Commit, true), short: commitHeader(e.Commit, false)}
}

// commitHeader returns the header lines of c: the bar, then the message, or
// with full unset its subject only, indented as git log does.
func commitHeader(c gitsrc.Commit, full bool) []string {
        h := []string{fmt.Sprintf("commit %s · %s · %s", c.Short, c.Author, c.Date), "", "    " + c.Subject}
        if full && c.Body != "" {
                h = append(h, "")
                for l := range strings.SplitSeq(c.Body, "\n") {
                        h = append(h, strings.TrimRight("    "+l, " "))
                }
        }
        return append(h, "")
}

func (f *Flow) Init() tea.Cmd { return nil }

// commitLines returns the header lines of part as currently shown.
func (f *Flow) commitLines(part *flowPart) []string {
        if f.cfg.Settings.FullMessages {
                return part.full
        }
        return part.short
}

func (f *Flow) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
        var cmd tea.Cmd
        switch msg := msg.(type) {
        case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
                f.keepScreenRow(func() {
                        f.width, f.height = msg.Width, msg.Height
                        f.relocate() // wrapped lines take other pieces
                })
        case tea.KeyPressMsg:
                cmd = f.key(msg.String())
        case findMsg:
                f.findText(int(msg))
        case settingChangedMsg:
                // Wrapped lines take other pieces, and end in line_end or ␍, which take
                // a column.
                f.keepScreenRow(f.relocate)
                if msg.action == "stay_in_commit" && f.inLog() && f.cfg.Settings.StayInCommit {
                        f.msg = "staying in commit " + f.parts[f.rows[f.cursor].part].commit.Short
                }
                if msg.problem != "" {
                        f.msg = msg.problem
                }
        }
        f.loadVisible()
        return f, cmd
}

func (f *Flow) key(k string) tea.Cmd {
        h := keyFile.key(flowView, k)
        if f.replacing != nil && h.action != "anchor" && h.action != "cancel_mark" {
                f.replacing.replace = nil
                f.replacing = nil
        }
        f.msg = ""
        if p := f.prefix; p != "" {
                f.prefix = ""
                switch {
                case !isMarkLetter(k):
                case p == "set_mark":
                        f.setMark(k)
                default:
                        f.gotoMark(k)
                }
                return nil
        }
        page := max(f.bodyHeight()-1, 1)
        if delta, paging, ok := motion(h, page, far); ok {
                if paging {
                        f.scroll(delta)
                } else {
                        f.moveCursor(delta)
                }
                return nil
        }
        switch h.action {
        case "quit":
                return tea.Quit
        case "flow":
                return toggleFlow
        case "to_files":
                return func() tea.Msg { return backMsg{} }
        case "pane":
                f.active = f.active.other()
        case "half_page":
                f.scroll(direction(h.i == 1) * (page / 2))
        case "scroll":
                if h.i == 0 {
                        f.xoff = max(f.xoff-8, 0)
                } else {
                        f.xoff += 8
                }
        case "change":
                if !f.search(direction(h.i == 0), f.changeStart, true) && f.msg == "" {
                        f.msg = "no more changes"
                }
        case "next_file":
                if f.search(direction(h.i == 0), f.isKind(flowFile, 0), true) {
                        f.barToTop()
                } else if f.msg == "" {
                        f.msg = ends(h.i == 0, "last file", "first file")
                }
        case "history":
                switch {
                case !f.inLog():
                case f.search(f.order.step(historyDir(h)), f.isKind(flowCommit, 0), false):
                        f.barToTop()
                default:
                        f.msg = ends(historyDir(h) > 0, "last commit", "first commit")
                }
        case "anchor", "cancel_mark", "fold", "fold_all":
                f.delegate(h.action)
        case "search":
                return askSearch
        case "match":
                f.findText(direction(h.i == 0))
        case "moved":
                f.jump()
        case "set_mark":
                f.prefix, f.msg = h.action, "set mark: press a letter"
        case "jump":
                f.prefix, f.msg = h.action, "jump to mark: press its letter"
        default:
                return pageKey(h, f.inLog())
        }
        return nil
}

// delegate runs an anchor or fold action on the diff of the file under the
// cursor.
func (f *Flow) delegate(action string) {
        if action == "cancel_mark" && f.replacing == nil && f.marked != nil && f.marked.pending != nil {
                // The mark may be in another file than the cursor.
                f.keepScreenRow(func() {
                        f.dropMark()
                        f.relocate()
                })
                f.msg = "mark cancelled"
                return
        }
        if len(f.rows) == 0 || f.rows[f.cursor].kind != flowDiff {
                if action != "cancel_mark" {
                        f.msg = "no line here"
                }
                return
        }
        r := f.rows[f.cursor]
        e := f.parts[r.part].app.files[r.file]
        d := e.diff
        if action == "anchor" && f.marked != d {
                f.dropMark()
        }
        var at spot
        f.keepScreenRow(func() {
                at, f.msg = d.act(action, spot{r.line, r.sub, f.active}, f.built)
                e.updateStats()
                f.build()
                if i := f.findDiff(r.part, r.file, at.v, at.sub); i >= 0 {
                        f.cursor = i
                }
        })
        f.active = at.side
        f.replacing = nil
        if d.replace != nil {
                f.replacing = d
        }
        if d.pending != nil {
                f.marked = d
        } else if f.marked == d {
                f.marked = nil
        }
}

// jump moves the cursor to the other end of the moved line under it, in this
// or another file of the commit (see jumpTo).
func (f *Flow) jump() {
        if len(f.rows) == 0 || f.rows[f.cursor].kind != flowDiff {
                f.msg = "not moved"
                return
        }
        r := f.rows[f.cursor]
        app := f.parts[r.part].app
        d := app.files[r.file].diff
        end, msg, ok := d.otherEnd(spot{r.line, r.sub, f.active}, f.built)
        f.msg = msg
        if !ok {
                return
        }
        i := app.fileByGroup(end.file)
        if i < 0 {
                return
        }
        at := app.files[i].diff.spotOf(end.side, end.line, f.built)
        f.jumpTo(f.findDiff(r.part, i, at.v, at.sub))
        f.active = at.side
}

// jumpTo moves the cursor to row i, unless it is -1, for a jump within the
// flow (g, '): the screen stays when i is on it, see jumpTop.
func (f *Flow) jumpTo(i int) {
        if i < 0 {
                return
        }
        from := f.cursor
        f.cursor = i
        f.top = jumpTop(from, i, f.top, f.bodyHeight(), len(f.rows))
}

// setMark sets jump mark letter on the new-file line under the cursor.
func (f *Flow) setMark(letter string) {
        if len(f.rows) == 0 || f.rows[f.cursor].kind != flowDiff {
                f.msg = "no line of the new file here"
                return
        }
        r := f.rows[f.cursor]
        f.msg = setMark(f.cfg, f.parts[r.part].app.files[r.file].diff, letter, r.line)
}

// gotoMark moves the cursor to the line of jump mark letter when its file is
// in the commit under the cursor, or outside the log among the files (see
// jumpTo).
func (f *Flow) gotoMark(letter string) {
        mk, ok := f.cfg.Mark(letter)
        if !ok {
                f.msg = "no mark " + letter
                return
        }
        if len(f.rows) == 0 || f.parts[f.rows[f.cursor].part].app == nil {
                f.msg = notInDiff(letter, mk)
                return
        }
        r := f.rows[f.cursor]
        app := f.parts[r.part].app
        i := app.fileByPath(mk.Path)
        if i < 0 {
                f.msg = notInDiff(letter, mk)
                return
        }
        d := app.files[i].diff
        line, note, ok := d.markLine(mk)
        if !ok {
                f.msg = markMsg(letter, "the new file is empty")
                return
        }
        at := d.reveal(right, line, f.built)
        f.relocate() // a fold may have opened: the cursor keeps its row
        f.jumpTo(f.findDiff(r.part, i, at.v, at.sub))
        f.active, f.msg = right, markMsg(letter, note)
}

// dropMark cancels a pending mark; the caller rebuilds the rows.
func (f *Flow) dropMark() {
        if d := f.marked; d != nil && d.pending != nil {
                d.pending = nil
                d.buildView()
        }
        f.marked = nil
}

// build computes the rows from the parts.
func (f *Flow) build() {
        f.rows = f.rows[:0]
        f.built = newShape(f.cfg, f.width)
        add := func(k flowKind, part, file, line int) {
                f.rows = append(f.rows, flowRow{k, part, file, line, 0})
        }
        for p, part := range f.parts {
                for i := range f.commitLines(part) {
                        add(flowCommit, p, -1, i)
                }
                if part.err != "" || (part.app != nil && len(part.app.files) == 0) {
                        add(flowNote, p, -1, 0)
                }
                if part.app == nil {
                        continue
                }
                for i, e := range part.app.files {
                        add(flowFile, p, i, 0)
                        if e.Binary {
                                add(flowNote, p, i, 0)
                                continue
                        }
                        screen, _ := part.app.diffOf(e).screenLines(f.built)
                        for _, s := range screen {
                                f.rows = append(f.rows, flowRow{flowDiff, p, i, s.v, s.sub})
                        }
                }
        }
        f.cursor = max(min(f.cursor, len(f.rows)-1), 0)
}

// refresh runs reload, which may reload files, calling its argument after
// each reload of a diff, and rebuilds the rows. The cursor stays on its
// screen row and, in a reloaded file, on its line.
func (f *Flow) refresh(reload func(after func(*fileDiff))) {
        var cur flowRow
        var d *fileDiff
        var row diff.Row
        reloads, lost := 0, false
        if f.cursor < len(f.rows) && f.rows[f.cursor].kind == flowDiff {
                cur = f.rows[f.cursor]
                d = f.parts[cur.part].app.files[cur.file].diff
                row, reloads = d.rows[d.view[cur.line].row], d.reloads
        }
        f.keepScreenRow(func() {
                // The cursor's row is followed through each reload: a diff may
                // reload both sides, and each reload maps only its own side.
                reload(func(rd *fileDiff) {
                        if rd != d || lost {
                                return
                        }
                        if i := d.rowAfterReload(row); i >= 0 {
                                row = d.rows[i]
                        } else {
                                lost = true
                        }
                })
                if d != nil {
                        // The files may have been listed again, in another order.
                        cur.file = slices.IndexFunc(f.parts[cur.part].app.files, func(e *entry) bool { return e.diff == d })
                }
                f.relocate()
                if d == nil || cur.file < 0 || d.reloads == reloads || lost {
                        return
                }
                if v := d.follow(row, false); v >= 0 {
                        if i := f.findDiff(cur.part, cur.file, v, cur.sub); i >= 0 {
                                f.cursor = i
                        }
                }
        })
}

// followFiles keeps the cursor on its file of part when the files were
// listed again, from old to new: on the file's row, or for a file that is
// gone on the bar of the next file left, else of the last. The rows are
// rebuilt next.
func (f *Flow) followFiles(part int, old, new []*entry) {
        if f.cursor >= len(f.rows) {
                return
        }
        r := &f.rows[f.cursor]
        if r.part != part || r.file < 0 {
                return
        }
        for i := r.file; i < len(old); i++ {
                if j := slices.Index(new, old[i]); j >= 0 {
                        if i == r.file {
                                r.file = j
                        } else {
                                *r = flowRow{flowFile, part, j, 0, 0}
                        }
                        return
                }
        }
        *r = flowRow{flowFile, part, len(new) - 1, 0, 0}
}

// followParts keeps the cursor on its commit when the commits were listed
// again, from the parts old to f.parts, which still hold it. The rows are
// rebuilt next.
func (f *Flow) followParts(old []*flowPart) {
        if f.cursor < len(f.rows) {
                r := &f.rows[f.cursor]
                r.part = slices.Index(f.parts, old[r.part])
        }
}

// relocate rebuilds the rows, keeping the cursor on its row: with wrapped
// lines on the same piece of the same line, else on the first line of its
// diff row when the layout changed.
func (f *Flow) relocate() {
        var cur flowRow
        ok := f.cursor < len(f.rows)
        if ok {
                cur = f.rows[f.cursor]
        }
        from := f.built
        f.build()
        i := f.find(cur)
        if i < 0 && cur.kind == flowCommit {
                // A message line no longer shown: on to the subject.
                i = f.find(flowRow{flowCommit, cur.part, -1, 2, 0})
        }
        if cur.kind == flowDiff {
                sub := cur.sub
                if d := f.parts[cur.part].app.files[cur.file].diff; cur.line < len(d.view) {
                        sub = d.convertSub(cur.line, cur.sub, from, f.built, f.active)
                }
                i = f.findDiff(cur.part, cur.file, cur.line, sub)
        }
        if ok && i >= 0 {
                f.cursor = i
        }
}

// findDiff returns the index of line sub of diff row line, or of its first
// line when it has no line sub, or -1.
func (f *Flow) findDiff(part, file, line, sub int) int {
        if i := f.find(flowRow{flowDiff, part, file, line, sub}); i >= 0 {
                return i
        }
        return f.find(flowRow{flowDiff, part, file, line, 0})
}

func (f *Flow) find(r flowRow) int {
        for i, x := range f.rows {
                if x == r {
                        return i
                }
        }
        return -1
}

// inLog reports whether f shows the commits of the log, rather than the
// files outside it.
func (f *Flow) inLog() bool { return f.load != nil }

func (f *Flow) unloaded(part int) bool {
        p := f.parts[part]
        return p.app == nil && p.err == ""
}

// unloadedIn returns a commit with rows in [a, b] that is not loaded yet, or
// -1.
func (f *Flow) unloadedIn(a, b int) int {
        if !f.inLog() {
                return -1
        }
        for i := max(a, 0); i <= min(b, len(f.rows)-1); i++ {
                if p := f.rows[i].part; f.unloaded(p) {
                        return p
                }
        }
        return -1
}

// loadPart loads the files of commit p; the cursor stays on its row and its
// screen row.
func (f *Flow) loadPart(p int) {
        part := f.parts[p]
        app, err := f.load(p)
        if err != nil {
                part.err = firstLine(err)
        } else {
                part.app = app
        }
        f.keepScreenRow(f.relocate)
}

// loadVisible loads the commits shown on screen.
func (f *Flow) loadVisible() {
        for {
                p := f.unloadedIn(f.top, f.top+f.bodyHeight()-1)
                if p < 0 {
                        return
                }
                f.loadPart(p)
        }
}

// moveCursor moves the cursor by delta rows. Commits on the way and a
// screen beyond are loaded first, so it lands where it would with all
// commits loaded; for far only those around where it lands, the others load
// as they come near.
func (f *Flow) moveCursor(delta int) {
        if max(delta, -delta) >= far {
                f.loadAroundEnd(delta)
        } else {
                f.loadAhead(delta)
        }
        f.cursor = f.confine(max(min(f.cursor+delta, len(f.rows)-1), 0))
        f.scrollToCursor()
}

// loadAroundEnd loads the commits within a screen of the first row the cursor
// may go to, for delta below 0, else of the last. Only home and end need it:
// should those keys go, as they may, this and the far branch of moveCursor go
// with their cases in motion.
func (f *Flow) loadAroundEnd(delta int) {
        h := f.bodyHeight()
        for {
                lo, hi := f.bounds()
                end := hi
                if delta < 0 {
                        end = lo
                }
                p := f.unloadedIn(end-h, end+h)
                if p < 0 {
                        return
                }
                f.loadPart(p)
        }
}

// bounds returns the first and last row the cursor may go to: those of its
// commit while stay_in_commit is set, else all.
func (f *Flow) bounds() (lo, hi int) {
        lo, hi = 0, len(f.rows)-1
        if !f.inLog() || !f.cfg.Settings.StayInCommit || len(f.rows) == 0 {
                return lo, hi
        }
        p := f.rows[f.cursor].part
        for lo = f.cursor; lo > 0 && f.rows[lo-1].part == p; lo-- {
        }
        for hi = f.cursor; hi < len(f.rows)-1 && f.rows[hi+1].part == p; hi++ {
        }
        return lo, hi
}

// confine returns row i, or the nearest row within the bounds with a
// warning that the cursor stays in its commit.
func (f *Flow) confine(i int) int {
        lo, hi := f.bounds()
        if i >= lo && i <= hi {
                return i
        }
        f.msg = "stays in commit " + f.parts[f.rows[f.cursor].part].commit.Short + " (C)"
        return max(min(i, hi), lo)
}

// scroll moves the window by delta rows, as far as it can, and the cursor
// along, so that the cursor keeps its screen row. Commits are loaded first,
// as for moveCursor.
func (f *Flow) scroll(delta int) {
        f.loadAhead(delta)
        top := f.top
        f.top = max(min(f.top+delta, len(f.rows)-f.bodyHeight()), 0)
        f.cursor = f.confine(max(min(f.cursor+f.top-top, len(f.rows)-1), 0))
        f.scrollToCursor()
}

// loadAhead loads the commits on the way of a cursor move by delta rows and
// a screen beyond.
func (f *Flow) loadAhead(delta int) {
        h := f.bodyHeight()
        for {
                lo, hi := f.cursor, f.cursor+delta+h
                if delta < 0 {
                        lo, hi = f.cursor+delta-h, f.cursor
                }
                p := f.unloadedIn(lo, hi)
                if p < 0 {
                        break
                }
                f.loadPart(p)
        }
}

// search moves the cursor to the next row in direction dir for which ok
// holds, loading commits on the way, and reports whether there is one. With
// stay set it does not leave the bounds; reaching them sets a warning.
func (f *Flow) search(dir int, ok func(int) bool, stay bool) bool {
        for {
                loaded := false
                for i := f.cursor + dir; i >= 0 && i < len(f.rows); i += dir {
                        if lo, hi := f.bounds(); stay && (i < lo || i > hi) {
                                f.confine(i)
                                return false
                        }
                        if p := f.rows[i].part; f.inLog() && f.unloaded(p) {
                                f.loadPart(p)
                                loaded = true
                                break
                        }
                        if ok(i) {
                                f.cursor = i
                                f.scrollToCursor()
                                return true
                        }
                }
                if !loaded {
                        return false
                }
        }
}

// barToTop scrolls the bar under the cursor to the top of the screen, so
// that what it starts is visible.
func (f *Flow) barToTop() {
        f.top = f.cursor
        f.scrollToCursor()
}

func (f *Flow) isKind(k flowKind, line int) func(int) bool {
        return func(i int) bool { return f.rows[i].kind == k && f.rows[i].line == line }
}

// changeStart reports whether row i starts a block of changed rows.
func (f *Flow) changeStart(i int) bool {
        changed := func(i int) bool {
                r := f.rows[i]
                return r.kind == flowDiff && f.parts[r.part].app.files[r.file].diff.changed(r.line)
        }
        return changed(i) && (i == 0 || !changed(i-1))
}

// show puts the cursor on line sub of diff row line of file in part, on
// the file bar for line -1 and on the commit bar for file -1, at screen row
// screenRow.
func (f *Flow) show(part, file, line, sub, screenRow int) {
        if f.inLog() && f.unloaded(part) {
                f.loadPart(part)
        }
        // While hidden, diff views may have changed the diffs or the layout.
        f.build()
        f.adopt(part, file)
        i := f.find(flowRow{flowCommit, part, -1, 0, 0})
        switch {
        case f.parts[part].app == nil:
        case file >= 0 && line >= 0:
                i = f.findDiff(part, file, line, sub)
        case file >= 0:
                i = f.find(flowRow{flowFile, part, file, 0, 0})
        }
        f.cursor = max(i, 0)
        f.top = f.cursor - screenRow
        f.scrollToCursor()
        f.loadVisible()
}

// showFrom shows file of part where its diff view from leaves it: on the
// cursor's line at its screen row, in its pane. For from nil it shows the
// file's bar, or the commit's bar for file -1. Model.takeOver is the way
// back.
func (f *Flow) showFrom(part, file int, from *Model) {
        if from == nil {
                f.show(part, file, -1, 0, 0)
                return
        }
        from.syncLayout()
        f.show(part, file, from.cursor, from.sub, from.pos()-from.top)
        f.active = from.active
}

// adopt takes over the pending mark and replace prompt that diff views of
// the files of part left, so that esc and enter treat them as the flow's
// own. Of several pending marks the one of file is kept, else the first;
// the others are dropped, as the flow keeps one.
func (f *Flow) adopt(part, file int) {
        f.marked, f.replacing = nil, nil
        app := f.parts[part].app
        if app == nil {
                return
        }
        var marked []*fileDiff
        for i, e := range app.files {
                d := e.diff
                if d == nil {
                        continue
                }
                if d.pending != nil {
                        if i == file {
                                marked = append([]*fileDiff{d}, marked...)
                        } else {
                                marked = append(marked, d)
                        }
                }
                if d.replace != nil {
                        f.replacing = d
                }
        }
        if len(marked) == 0 {
                return
        }
        for _, d := range marked[1:] {
                f.marked = d
                f.dropMark()
        }
        f.marked = marked[0]
        if len(marked) > 1 {
                f.build() // the dropped marks may have folded rows
        }
}

// position returns the part of the cursor, its file (-1 on rows of the
// commit) and the line in the file's diff view (-1 on other rows).
func (f *Flow) position() (part, file, line int) {
        if len(f.rows) == 0 {
                return 0, -1, -1
        }
        r := f.rows[f.cursor]
        line = -1
        if r.kind == flowDiff {
                line = r.line
        }
        return r.part, r.file, line
}

// headerLines is the number of lines above the rows: the commit in the log,
// and the file names.
func (f *Flow) headerLines() int {
        if f.inLog() {
                return 2
        }
        return 1
}

// cursorSub returns the line of the cursor within its diff row.
func (f *Flow) cursorSub() int {
        if len(f.rows) == 0 {
                return 0
        }
        return f.rows[f.cursor].sub
}

func (f *Flow) bodyHeight() int { return max(f.height-1-f.headerLines(), 1) }

func (f *Flow) keepScreenRow(fn func()) {
        offset := f.cursor - f.top
        fn()
        f.top = f.cursor - offset
        f.scrollToCursor()
}

func (f *Flow) scrollToCursor() {
        f.top = scrollWindow(f.cursor, f.top, f.bodyHeight(), len(f.rows))
}

// help returns the keys of the status bar.
func (f *Flow) help() string {
        return statusKeys(f.cfg, barKeys{enter: "anchor", back: "to_files", commits: f.inLog(), flow: true})
}

func (f *Flow) View() tea.View { return tea.NewView(f.frame()) }

func (f *Flow) frame() string {
        if f.width == 0 {
                return ""
        }
        var b strings.Builder
        b.WriteString(f.header())
        for i := f.top; i < f.top+f.bodyHeight(); i++ {
                if i < len(f.rows) {
                        b.WriteString(f.row(i))
                }
                b.WriteString("\n")
        }
        b.WriteString(f.status())
        return b.String()
}

// header renders the lines above the rows: in the log the commit of the
// cursor, then the names of its file as in the single file view.
func (f *Flow) header() string {
        t := f.cfg.Theme
        p, file, _ := f.position()
        var b strings.Builder
        if f.inLog() {
                c := f.parts[p].commit
                line := " " + t.CommitHash.Render(c.Short) + " " + t.Title.Render(c.Subject)
                b.WriteString(ansi.Truncate(line, f.width, "…") + "\n")
        }
        if file >= 0 {
                b.WriteString(paneTitles(t, f.parts[p].app.files[file].paneNames(), f.active, f.cfg.Settings.SideBySide, f.width))
        }
        return b.String() + "\n"
}

func (f *Flow) status() string {
        p, file, _ := f.position()
        var where []string
        if f.inLog() {
                c := fmt.Sprintf("commit %d/%d", p+1, len(f.parts))
                if f.cfg.Settings.StayInCommit {
                        c += " (C)"
                }
                where = append(where, c)
        }
        if file >= 0 {
                files := f.parts[p].app.files
                where = append(where, fmt.Sprintf("file %d/%d", file+1, len(files)))
                if d := files[file].diff; d != nil {
                        where = append(where, fmt.Sprintf("anchors %d", len(d.anchors)))
                }
        }
        msg := f.msg
        if msg == "" && f.marked != nil {
                msg = f.marked.markPrompt()
        }
        left := pageStatus(f.cfg, msg, f.help())
        return statusLine(f.cfg.Theme, f.width, left, " "+strings.Join(where, " · "))
}

// row renders row i. Diff rows are drawn by the file's diff with the flow's
// pane, scroll offset and width; other rows show the cursor in the first
// column and in their text, in the cursor style.
func (f *Flow) row(i int) string {
        t := f.cfg.Theme
        r := f.rows[i]
        part := f.parts[r.part]
        cursor := i == f.cursor
        mark := " "
        if cursor {
                mark = t.Cursor.Render(" ")
        }
        w := max(f.width-1, 0)
        // bar renders s across the row in st.
        bar := func(s string, st lipgloss.Style) string {
                s = ansi.Truncate(s, w, "…")
                if cursor {
                        st = t.Cursor.Inherit(st)
                }
                return st.Render(s + strings.Repeat(" ", max(w-ansi.StringWidth(s), 0)))
        }
        // text renders s in st, on the cursor row past its indentation.
        text := func(s string, st lipgloss.Style) string {
                s = ansi.Truncate(s, w, "…")
                if !cursor {
                        return st.Render(s)
                }
                body := strings.TrimLeft(s, " ")
                return st.Render(s[:len(s)-len(body)]) + t.Cursor.Inherit(st).Render(body)
        }

        switch r.kind {
        case flowCommit:
                line := f.commitLines(part)[r.line]
                switch r.line {
                case 0:
                        return mark + bar(" "+line, t.CommitBar)
                case 2:
                        return mark + text(line, t.Zero.Bold(true))
                }
                return mark + text(line, t.Zero)
        case flowFile:
                e := part.app.files[r.file]
                line := " "
                if lbl := e.label(t); lbl != "" {
                        line += lbl + " "
                }
                line += e.name()
                if e.Binary {
                        line += "   binary"
                } else if e.added > 0 || e.removed > 0 {
                        line += fmt.Sprintf("   +%d -%d", e.added, e.removed)
                }
                return mark + bar(line, t.FileBar)
        case flowNote:
                note := "no changed files"
                switch {
                case r.file >= 0:
                        note = "binary file"
                case part.err != "":
                        note = printable(part.err)
                }
                return mark + text("  "+note, t.Hint)
        }
        d := part.app.files[r.file].diff
        return d.render(sline{r.line, r.sub}, i == f.cursor, layout{f.width, f.xoff, f.active, f.built})
}

package ui

import (
        "fmt"
        "strings"

        tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
        "charm.land/lipgloss/v2"
        "github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"

        "jdiff/internal/config"
)

// binding is a key and what it does; a binding without key is a heading.
type binding struct{ key, what string }

// bindings lists the lines of the help popup, from keybindings.toml.
var bindings = keyFile.bindings()

// bindings returns the lines of the help popup.
func (p keyPages) bindings() []binding {
        var bs []binding
        for _, h := range p.Help {
                switch {
                case h.Heading != "":
                        bs = append(bs, binding{"", h.Heading})
                case h.Note != "":
                        bs = append(bs, binding{"", "  " + p.keyText(h.Note)})
                default:
                        r := p.row(h.Action)
                        bs = append(bs, binding{strings.Join(r.Keys, " "), p.keyText(r.Help)})
                }
        }
        return bs
}

// Help wraps the top-level model and shows the key bindings of all pages in
// a popup over it on ?.
type Help struct {
        tea.Model
        cfg           *config.Config
        open          bool
        top           int // first shown line of the popup
        width, height int
}

// WithHelp returns m with the help popup.
func WithHelp(cfg *config.Config, m tea.Model) *Help { return &Help{Model: m, cfg: cfg} }

func (h *Help) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
        switch msg := msg.(type) {
        case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
                h.width, h.height = msg.Width, msg.Height
                h.scroll(0)
        case tea.KeyPressMsg:
                if cmd, ok := popupKeys(msg, h.open, "help", h.key); ok {
                        return h, cmd
                }
        }
        _, cmd := h.Model.Update(msg)
        return h, cmd
}

// popupKeys passes the key press msg to key, when the popup is open or msg
// opens it, being a key of action opener, and reports whether it did; else
// msg is for the page below.
func popupKeys(msg tea.KeyPressMsg, open bool, opener string, key func(string) tea.Cmd) (tea.Cmd, bool) {
        k := msg.String()
        if !open && !isAction(k, opener) {
                return nil, false
        }
        return key(k), true
}

// key handles k in the popup, or opens it on ?.
func (h *Help) key(k string) tea.Cmd {
        if !h.open {
                h.open, h.top = true, 0
                return nil
        }
        if isAction(k, "help") {
                h.open = false
                return nil
        }
        switch k {
        case "esc", "q":
                h.open = false
        case "ctrl+c":
                return tea.Quit
        case "down", "j":
                h.scroll(1)
        case "up", "k":
                h.scroll(-1)
        }
        return nil
}

// boxHeight is the height of the popup.
func (h *Help) boxHeight() int { return popupHeight(len(bindings), h.height) }

// scroll moves the shown lines by delta; from the top, it moves on by two
// at once, as the window never starts at the second line.
func (h *Help) scroll(delta int) {
        top := h.top + delta
        if top == 1 && delta > 0 {
                top = 2
        }
        h.top = windowAt(top, h.boxHeight()-2, len(bindings)).first
}

func (h *Help) View() tea.View { return tea.NewView(h.frame()) }

func (h *Help) frame() string {
        base := h.Model.View().Content
        if !h.open || h.width == 0 {
                return base
        }
        t := h.cfg.Theme
        keyW, whatW := 0, 0
        for _, b := range bindings {
                keyW = max(keyW, ansi.StringWidth(b.key))
                whatW = max(whatW, ansi.StringWidth(b.what))
        }
        line := func(i int) string {
                switch b := bindings[i]; {
                case b.key == "" && !strings.HasPrefix(b.what, " "):
                        return t.Branch.Render(b.what)
                case b.key == "":
                        return strings.Repeat(" ", keyW+2) + t.Hint.Render(strings.TrimSpace(b.what))
                default:
                        return lipgloss.NewStyle().Bold(true).Render(fmt.Sprintf("  %-*s", keyW, b.key)) + "  " + b.what
                }
        }
        lines := windowAt(h.top, h.boxHeight()-2, len(bindings)).rows(t, len(bindings), line)
        w := min(keyW+whatW+8, h.width) // the key indent, gap, border and padding
        title := t.CommitHash.Render(" keys ")
        if h.boxHeight()-2 < len(bindings) {
                title += t.Hint.Render(fmt.Sprintf(" %d/%d ", h.top+1, len(bindings)))
        }
        return overlay(base, box(t, w, h.boxHeight(), title, lines), w, h.width)
}

// overlay draws the popup pop, w columns wide, centered over base, which
// is width columns wide.
func overlay(base, pop string, w, width int) string {
        y := max((strings.Count(base, "\n")+1-strings.Count(strings.TrimSuffix(pop, "\n"), "\n")-1)/2, 0)
        return overlayAt(base, pop, max((width-w)/2, 0), y, w, width)
}

// overlayAt draws the popup pop, w columns wide, over base, which is width
// columns wide, from column x of row y on.
func overlayAt(base, pop string, x, y, w, width int) string {
        rows := strings.Split(base, "\n")
        popRows := strings.Split(strings.TrimSuffix(pop, "\n"), "\n")
        for i, p := range popRows {
                if y+i >= len(rows) {
                        break
                }
                row := rows[y+i]
                left := ansi.Truncate(row, x, "")
                left += strings.Repeat(" ", x-ansi.StringWidth(left))
                rows[y+i] = left + p + ansi.Cut(row, x+w, width)
        }
        return strings.Join(rows, "\n")
}

package ui

import (
        "fmt"
        "slices"
        "strings"

        tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
        "charm.land/lipgloss/v2"
        "github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"

        "jdiff/internal/config"
        "jdiff/internal/gitsrc"
        "jdiff/internal/source"
)

// LoadFunc returns the files a commit changed.
type LoadFunc func(gitsrc.Commit) ([]source.File, error)

// CountFunc returns the line counts of the commits with the given hashes.
type CountFunc func(hashes []string) (map[string]gitsrc.LineCounts, error)

// countsMsg brings the line counts of the commits hashes, or why they could
// not be counted.
type countsMsg struct {
        hashes []string
        counts map[string]gitsrc.LineCounts
        err    error
}

// countWidth is the width the count columns keep while counts arrive, so
// that they do not move with each batch.
const countWidth = 4

// commitEntry is one commit in the log. Its file selector is created on
// first open and kept, so anchors survive going back to the list.
type commitEntry struct {
        gitsrc.Commit
        counted bool // Added and Removed are known
        app     *App
}

// Log is the Bubble Tea model of the commit list. Enter shows the files of
// a commit; N/P step through commits from the deeper views.
type Log struct {
        cfg    *config.Config
        branch string // shown above the commit list
        load   LoadFunc
        // counts counts the lines of the commits shown, in the background; nil
        // when the commits came counted or counting failed.
        counts   CountFunc
        relist   CommitsFunc             // lists the commits again, nil when they cannot be
        counting bool                    // a count is running
        byHash   map[string]*commitEntry // the commits by hash
        minCount int                     // the least width of the count columns

        commits []*commitEntry
        order   historyOrder // of commits
        list
        open   *commitEntry // commit whose files are shown, nil for the list
        files  bool         // in flow mode: the file list of open, not the flow view
        flow   *Flow        // all commits one after the other
        msg    string
        prefix string // "'" while waiting for the letter of a mark
}

// NewLog returns the commit list for commits of branch, in the order of git
// log: newest first, or with oldestFirst (git log --reverse) oldest. With
// counts, their line counts are counted once they are shown; without, they
// come with the commits.
func NewLog(cfg *config.Config, branch string, commits []gitsrc.Commit, oldestFirst bool,
        load LoadFunc, counts CountFunc) *Log {
        l := &Log{cfg: cfg, branch: branch, load: load, counts: counts, byHash: map[string]*commitEntry{}, minCount: 1,
                order: orderOf(oldestFirst)}
        if counts != nil {
                l.minCount = countWidth
        }
        var parts []*flowPart
        for _, c := range commits {
                e := newCommitEntry(c, counts == nil)
                l.commits = append(l.commits, e)
                l.byHash[c.Hash] = e
                parts = append(parts, newCommitPart(e))
        }
        l.list = list{n: len(l.commits), preview: &cfg.Settings.Preview}
        l.flow = newFlow(cfg, parts, l.app)
        l.flow.order = l.order
        if len(commits) == 0 {
                l.msg = "no commits"
        }
        if w := cfg.Warning(); w != "" {
                l.msg = w
        }
        return l
}

// newCommitEntry returns the entry of c, counted when its line counts are
// known.
func newCommitEntry(c gitsrc.Commit, counted bool) *commitEntry {
        // Commits are only shown: their text is made printable once.
        c.Author, c.Subject = printable(c.Author), printable(c.Subject)
        body := strings.Split(c.Body, "\n")
        for i, line := range body {
                body[i] = printable(line)
        }
        c.Body = strings.Join(body, "\n")
        return &commitEntry{Commit: c, counted: counted}
}

// CommitsFunc lists the commits of the log again, with the branch.
type CommitsFunc func() (branch string, commits []gitsrc.Commit, err error)

// WithRelist sets how r lists the commits again and returns l.
func (l *Log) WithRelist(list CommitsFunc) *Log {
        l.relist = list
        return l
}

func (l *Log) Init() tea.Cmd { return nil }

func (l *Log) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
        if m, ok := msg.(countsMsg); ok {
                l.addCounts(m)
        } else if cmd := l.update(msg); cmd != nil {
                return l, cmd
        }
        return l, l.countShown()
}

// countShown returns the command counting the lines of the commits on the
// list's screen and a screen beyond that are not counted yet, or nil when
// they are, a count is running or the list is not shown.
func (l *Log) countShown() tea.Cmd {
        if l.counts == nil || l.counting || l.open != nil || l.height == 0 {
                return nil
        }
        var hashes []string
        for i := l.top; i < min(l.top+2*l.listHeight(), len(l.commits)); i++ {
                if e := l.commits[i]; !e.counted {
                        hashes = append(hashes, e.Hash)
                }
        }
        if len(hashes) == 0 {
                return nil
        }
        l.counting = true
        counts := l.counts
        return func() tea.Msg {
                c, err := counts(hashes)
                return countsMsg{hashes, c, err}
        }
}

// addCounts takes the line counts of m. After an error the counts are not
// tried again: the list stays without them.
func (l *Log) addCounts(m countsMsg) {
        l.counting = false
        if m.err != nil {
                l.counts = nil
                l.setMsg("line counts: " + firstLine(m.err))
                return
        }
        for _, h := range m.hashes {
                if e := l.byHash[h]; e != nil {
                        c := m.counts[h]
                        e.Added, e.Removed, e.counted = c.Added, c.Removed, true
                }
        }
}

func (l *Log) update(msg tea.Msg) tea.Cmd {
        switch msg := msg.(type) {
        case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
                l.width, l.height = msg.Width, msg.Height
                l.scrollToCursor()
                if l.flowShown() {
                        l.flow.Update(msg)
                        return nil
                }
        case settingChangedMsg:
                l.scrollToCursor() // the message box takes room from the list
                if msg.problem != "" && l.open == nil {
                        l.msg = msg.problem
                }
        case backMsg:
                if l.flowShown() {
                        l.filesOfFlow()
                } else {
                        l.open, l.files = nil, false
                }
                return nil
        case flowAtMsg:
                l.showFlow(int(msg), nil)
                return nil
        case switchCommitMsg:
                l.switchCommit(int(msg))
                return nil
        case refreshMsg:
                l.relistCommits(msg.quiet)
                return nil
        case showMsg:
                l.setMsg(string(msg))
                return nil
        case toggleUntrackedMsg:
                l.setMsg(untrackedOnlyInDiff)
                return nil
        case toggleFlowMsg:
                l.toggleFlow()
                if w := saveState(l.cfg); w != "" {
                        l.setMsg(w)
                }
                return nil
        }
        if l.flowShown() {
                _, cmd := l.flow.Update(msg)
                l.cursor, _, _ = l.flow.position()
                l.scrollToCursor()
                return cmd
        }
        if l.open != nil {
                _, cmd := l.open.app.Update(msg)
                return cmd
        }
        if k, ok := msg.(tea.KeyPressMsg); ok {
                return l.key(k.String())
        }
        return nil
}

func (l *Log) key(k string) tea.Cmd {
        l.msg = ""
        if l.prefix != "" {
                // Marks are lines of files: the list has none to jump to.
                l.prefix = ""
                if isMarkLetter(k) {
                        l.msg = "mark " + k + ": open a commit to jump to it"
                }
                return nil
        }
        h := keyFile.key(commitList, k)
        page := max(l.listHeight()-1, 1)
        if delta, _, ok := motion(h, page, len(l.commits)); ok {
                l.moveCursor(delta)
                return nil
        }
        switch h.action {
        case "quit":
                return tea.Quit
        case "open":
                if l.cfg.Settings.Flow {
                        l.showFlow(-1, nil)
                } else {
                        l.openCommit(l.cursor, "")
                }
        case "flow":
                return toggleFlow
        case "history":
                // The next or previous commit in history, as N and P go from its files.
                dir := historyDir(h)
                if i := l.cursor + l.order.step(dir); i >= 0 && i < len(l.commits) {
                        l.moveCursor(i - l.cursor)
                } else {
                        l.msg = ends(dir > 0, "last commit", "first commit")
                }
        case "jump":
                l.prefix, l.msg = h.action, "jump to mark: press its letter"
        default:
                return pageKey(h, false)
        }
        return nil
}

// flowShown reports whether the flow view is shown.
func (l *Log) flowShown() bool { return l.open != nil && l.cfg.Settings.Flow && !l.files }

// showFlow shows the flow view on file of the commit under the cursor,
// where its diff view from leaves it, or on its bar for from nil and on the
// commit's bar for file -1 (see Flow.showFrom).
func (l *Log) showFlow(file int, from *Model) {
        l.open, l.files = l.commits[l.cursor], false
        l.flow.width, l.flow.height = l.width, l.height
        l.flow.showFrom(l.cursor, file, from)
}

// toggleFlow switches between the flow view and the files of the commit
// under the cursor, keeping the cursor on its line and screen row.
func (l *Log) toggleFlow() {
        st := &l.cfg.Settings
        if !st.Flow {
                st.Flow = true
                switch {
                case l.open == nil:
                        l.showFlow(-1, nil)
                case l.open.app.open == nil:
                        l.showFlow(l.open.app.cursor, nil)
                default:
                        l.showFlow(l.open.app.cursor, l.open.app.open.view)
                }
                return
        }
        st.Flow = false
        if l.open == nil {
                return
        }
        if l.files { // the file list stays
                l.files = false
                return
        }
        p, file, line := l.flow.position()
        screenRow := l.flow.cursor - l.flow.top
        path := ""
        if e := l.commits[p]; e.app != nil && file >= 0 {
                path = e.app.files[file].Path
        }
        l.open = nil
        if !l.openCommit(p, path) {
                return
        }
        app := l.open.app
        if app.open == nil && file >= 0 {
                app.cursor = file
                app.scrollToCursor()
        }
        if app.open != nil && app.open.Path == path {
                app.open.view.takeOver(l.flow, line, screenRow)
        }
}

// app returns the file selector of commit i, loading its files on first
// use.
func (l *Log) app(i int) (*App, error) {
        e := l.commits[i]
        if e.app == nil {
                files, err := l.load(e.Commit)
                if err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                e.app = newApp(l.cfg, files, &e.Commit)
        }
        return e.app, nil
}

// openCommit shows the files of commit i, opening path directly when the
// commit changed it, and the only file of a single-file commit.
func (l *Log) openCommit(i int, path string) bool {
        if i < 0 || i >= len(l.commits) {
                return false
        }
        e := l.commits[i]
        if _, err := l.app(i); err != nil {
                l.msg = firstLine(err)
                return false
        }
        e.app.Update(tea.WindowSizeMsg{Width: l.width, Height: l.height})
        switch {
        case path != "" && e.app.openPath(path):
        case e.app.single():
                e.app.cursor = 0
                e.app.openFile()
        default:
                e.app.open = nil
        }
        l.open = e
        l.cursor = i
        l.scrollToCursor()
        return true
}

// switchCommit shows the next (dir > 0) or previous commit in history,
// keeping the open file when the other commit changed it too.
func (l *Log) switchCommit(dir int) {
        if l.open == nil {
                return
        }
        app := l.open.app
        path := ""
        if app.open != nil {
                path = app.open.Path
        }
        i := l.cursor + l.order.step(dir)
        if i < 0 || i >= len(l.commits) {
                app.setMsg(ends(dir > 0, "last commit", "first commit"))
                return
        }
        if app.open != nil {
                app.open.updateStats()
        }
        if !l.openCommit(i, path) {
                app.setMsg(l.msg)
        }
}

// relistCommits lists the commits again (r). New commits are added and gone
// ones dropped; a commit still there keeps its entry, and so its files with
// their anchors and folds: its content cannot change. The cursor stays on
// its commit; when the commit shown is gone, the list is shown. With quiet, it
// does not say when nothing changed.
func (l *Log) relistCommits(quiet bool) {
        if l.relist == nil {
                l.setMsg("nothing to load again")
                return
        }
        branch, commits, err := l.relist()
        if err != nil {
                l.setMsg(firstLine(err))
                return
        }
        parts := map[*commitEntry]*flowPart{}
        for _, p := range l.flow.parts {
                parts[p.commit] = p
        }
        var cur *commitEntry
        if l.cursor < len(l.commits) {
                cur = l.commits[l.cursor]
        }
        kept, oldParts, shown, open := l.byHash, l.flow.parts, l.flowShown(), l.open
        if shown {
                p, _, _ := l.flow.position()
                open = oldParts[p].commit
        }
        l.branch, l.commits, l.flow.parts, l.byHash = branch, nil, nil, map[string]*commitEntry{}
        added := 0
        for _, c := range commits {
                e, p := kept[c.Hash], (*flowPart)(nil)
                if e != nil {
                        delete(kept, c.Hash)
                        p = parts[e]
                } else {
                        e = newCommitEntry(c, l.counts == nil)
                        p = newCommitPart(e)
                        added++
                }
                l.commits = append(l.commits, e)
                l.byHash[c.Hash] = e
                l.flow.parts = append(l.flow.parts, p)
        }
        l.list.n = len(l.commits)
        if i := slices.Index(l.commits, cur); i >= 0 {
                l.cursor = i
        } else {
                l.cursor = max(min(l.cursor, len(l.commits)-1), 0)
        }
        msg := refreshed(added, len(kept), 0, 0)
        switch {
        case open != nil && kept[open.Hash] == open:
                msg = "commit " + open.Short + " is gone; " + msg
                l.open = nil
        case shown:
                l.flow.followParts(oldParts)
                l.flow.keepScreenRow(l.flow.relocate)
                l.flow.loadVisible()
                l.cursor, _, _ = l.flow.position()
                l.open = l.commits[l.cursor]
        case open != nil:
                l.cursor = slices.Index(l.commits, open)
        }
        l.scrollToCursor()
        if !quiet || msg != nothingChanged {
                l.setMsg(msg)
        }
}

// filesOfFlow shows the file list of the commit under the flow view's
// cursor, on its file, as backspace does from a diff view. A commit of one
// file, or one that cannot be loaded, has no list to show: the commits are
// shown, on it.
func (l *Log) filesOfFlow() {
        p, file, _ := l.flow.position()
        l.cursor = p
        l.scrollToCursor()
        app, err := l.app(p)
        if err != nil || app.single() {
                l.open, l.files = nil, false
                return
        }
        app.Update(tea.WindowSizeMsg{Width: l.width, Height: l.height})
        app.open = nil
        if file >= 0 {
                app.cursor = file
                app.scrollToCursor()
        }
        l.open, l.files = l.commits[p], true
}

// setMsg shows msg in the status bar of whatever is displayed.
func (l *Log) setMsg(msg string) {
        switch {
        case l.flowShown():
                l.flow.msg = msg
        case l.open != nil:
                l.open.app.setMsg(msg)
        default:
                l.msg = msg
        }
}

// maxAuthorWidth caps the author column.
const maxAuthorWidth = 16

func (l *Log) View() tea.View { return tea.NewView(l.frame()) }

func (l *Log) frame() string {
        if l.flowShown() {
                return l.flow.frame()
        }
        if l.open != nil {
                return l.open.app.frame()
        }
        if l.width == 0 {
                return ""
        }
        t := l.cfg.Theme
        addW, remW, authW, subjW, fixedW := l.minCount, l.minCount, 0, 0, 0
        for _, c := range l.commits {
                addW = max(addW, len(fmt.Sprint(c.Added)))
                remW = max(remW, len(fmt.Sprint(c.Removed)))
                authW = max(authW, min(ansi.StringWidth(c.Author), maxAuthorWidth))
                subjW = max(subjW, ansi.StringWidth(c.Subject))
                fixedW = max(fixedW, ansi.StringWidth(c.Short)+ansi.StringWidth(c.Date))
        }
        // The subject gets the room left by the other columns, so that author
        // and date stay aligned and visible.
        fixedW += 1 + 2 + (addW + 1) + 1 + (remW + 1) + 2 + 2 + authW + 2
        subjW = max(min(subjW, l.width-fixedW), 1)

        head := " " + t.Branch.Render(l.branch) + " " +
                lipgloss.NewStyle().Bold(true).Render(fmt.Sprintf("(%d commits)", len(l.commits)))
        item := func(i int, st func(lipgloss.Style) lipgloss.Style) string {
                c := l.commits[i]
                author := pad(ansi.Truncate(c.Author, maxAuthorWidth, "…"), authW, lipgloss.NewStyle())
                subject := ansi.Truncate(c.Subject, subjW, "…")
                subject += strings.Repeat(" ", subjW-ansi.StringWidth(subject))
                return " " + st(t.CommitHash).Render(c.Short) + "  " + counts(t, st, c.Added, c.Removed, addW, remW) + "  " +
                        st(lipgloss.NewStyle()).Render(subject) + "  " + st(t.Decoration).Render(author) + "  " +
                        st(t.Decoration).Render(c.Date)
        }
        hints := statusKeys(l.cfg, barKeys{enter: "open", flow: true})
        return l.render(t, head, item, func(h int) string { return l.messageBox(l.commits[l.cursor].Commit, h) }, pageStatus(l.cfg, l.msg, hints))
}

// messageBox renders the full message of c in a box height lines high,
// with the hash in the top border. A last … line marks a cut message.
func (l *Log) messageBox(c gitsrc.Commit, height int) string {
        t := l.cfg.Theme
        lines := []string{t.Zero.Bold(true).Render(c.Subject)}
        if c.Body != "" {
                lines = append(lines, "")
                for line := range strings.SplitSeq(c.Body, "\n") {
                        lines = append(lines, t.Zero.Render(line))
                }
        }
        if n := height - 2; len(lines) > n {
                lines = lines[:n]
                if n > 1 {
                        lines[n-1] = t.Hint.Render("…")
                }
        }
        return box(t, l.width, height, t.CommitHash.Render(" "+c.Hash+" "), lines)
}

package ui

import (
        "fmt"
        "strings"
        "unicode"

        "github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"
        "github.com/yuin/goldmark"
        "github.com/yuin/goldmark/ast"
        "github.com/yuin/goldmark/extension"
        east "github.com/yuin/goldmark/extension/ast"
        "github.com/yuin/goldmark/text"
)

// docStyle is what a piece of a page is, which decides its style in the
// theme when it is drawn.
type docStyle int

const (
        docPlain docStyle = iota
        docBold
        docItalic
        docCode
        docHeading
        docLink
        docDecoration // bullets, rules, the lines of tables and quotes
        docOverflow   // » at the end of a cut line
)

// docSeg is a piece of a line of a page in one style: printable text, part
// of link number link of the page, -1 for none.
type docSeg struct {
        text  string
        style docStyle
        link  int
}

// docLine is a line of a page; anchor is the name of the heading it starts,
// as links to it write it after #.
type docLine struct {
        segs   []docSeg
        anchor string
}

// plain returns the text of l, without styles.
func (l docLine) plain() string {
        var b strings.Builder
        for _, s := range l.segs {
                b.WriteString(s.text)
        }
        return b.String()
}

// docPage is a markdown page rendered for a width: its lines, none wider,
// and the targets of its links with the line each starts on.
type docPage struct {
        lines   []docLine
        links   []pageLink
        anchors map[string]int // the line of each heading
}

// pageLink is a link of a page: where it goes, as written, and its first
// line; broken when it leads nowhere, which Doc decides.
type pageLink struct {
        target string
        line   int
        broken bool
}

var markdown = goldmark.New(goldmark.WithExtensions(extension.Table))

// renderMarkdown renders src for a screen width columns wide. Images are left
// out, so a line of badges is gone; a line still too wide, of code or a table,
// is cut with ».
func renderMarkdown(src []byte, width int) docPage {
        width = max(width, 1)
        r := &mdRenderer{src: src}
        lines := r.blocks(markdown.Parser().Parse(text.NewReader(src)), width, false)
        page := docPage{anchors: map[string]int{}}
        for i := range r.links {
                page.links = append(page.links, pageLink{target: r.links[i], line: -1})
        }
        for i, l := range lines {
                l.segs = cutSegs(l.segs, width)
                lines[i] = l
                if l.anchor != "" {
                        page.anchors[l.anchor] = i
                }
                for _, s := range l.segs {
                        if s.link >= 0 && page.links[s.link].line < 0 {
                                page.links[s.link].line = i
                        }
                }
        }
        page.lines = lines
        return page
}

// renderText renders a file that is no markdown for a screen width columns
// wide: its lines as code, the long ones wrapped at the last space that
// fits, past their indentation, else where the line ends; the further pieces
// indented.
func renderText(src []byte, width int) docPage {
        width = max(width, 1)
        page := docPage{anchors: map[string]int{}}
        text := strings.TrimSuffix(strings.ReplaceAll(string(src), "\r\n", "\n"), "\n")
        for _, line := range strings.Split(text, "\n") {
                rest, lead, w := printable(line), "", width
                add := func(s string) {
                        page.lines = append(page.lines, docLine{segs: trimSegs([]docSeg{{lead, docPlain, -1}, {s, docCode, -1}})})
                }
                for ansi.StringWidth(rest) > w {
                        piece := ansi.Truncate(rest, w, "")
                        if i := strings.LastIndex(piece, " "); i > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(piece[:i]) != "" {
                                piece = piece[:i+1]
                        }
                        add(piece)
                        rest = rest[len(piece):]
                        if lead == "" && width > 2 {
                                lead, w = "  ", width-2
                        }
                }
                add(rest)
        }
        return page
}

// mdRenderer renders the blocks of a markdown tree into lines.
type mdRenderer struct {
        src     []byte
        links   []string        // the targets of the links found so far
        anchors map[string]bool // the anchors given so far
        depth   int             // of nested lists
}

// blocks renders the children of n one below the other, with an empty line
// between them unless tight, as in the items of a tight list.
func (r *mdRenderer) blocks(n ast.Node, width int, tight bool) []docLine {
        var out []docLine
        for c := n.FirstChild(); c != nil; c = c.NextSibling() {
                lines := r.block(c, width)
                if len(lines) == 0 {
                        continue
                }
                if len(out) > 0 && !tight {
                        out = append(out, docLine{})
                }
                out = append(out, lines...)
        }
        return out
}

// block renders the block n, width columns wide but for code.
func (r *mdRenderer) block(n ast.Node, width int) []docLine {
        switch n := n.(type) {
        case *ast.Heading:
                lines := wrapSegs(r.inline(n, docHeading), width)
                if len(lines) == 0 {
                        return nil
                }
                lines[0].anchor = r.anchor(n)
                if n.Level <= 2 {
                        w := 0
                        for _, l := range lines {
                                w = max(w, segsWidth(l.segs))
                        }
                        rule := "─"
                        if n.Level == 1 {
                                rule = "═"
                        }
                        lines = append(lines, docLine{segs: []docSeg{{strings.Repeat(rule, w), docDecoration, -1}}})
                }
                return lines
        case *ast.Paragraph, *ast.TextBlock:
                return wrapSegs(r.inline(n, docPlain), width)
        case *ast.List:
                return r.list(n, width)
        case *ast.FencedCodeBlock, *ast.CodeBlock:
                var out []docLine
                lines := n.Lines()
                for i := range lines.Len() {
                        seg := lines.At(i)
                        line := printable(strings.TrimRight(string(seg.Value(r.src)), "\r\n"))
                        out = append(out, docLine{segs: trimSegs([]docSeg{{"  ", docPlain, -1}, {line, docCode, -1}})})
                }
                return out
        case *ast.Blockquote:
                inner := r.blocks(n, max(width-2, 1), false)
                for i := range inner {
                        inner[i].segs = append([]docSeg{{"│ ", docDecoration, -1}}, inner[i].segs...)
                }
                return inner
        case *ast.ThematicBreak:
                return []docLine{{segs: []docSeg{{strings.Repeat("─", width), docDecoration, -1}}}}
        case *east.Table:
                return r.table(n, width)
        }
        return nil // HTML
}

// bullets are the marks of the items of unordered lists, by depth.
var bullets = []string{"•", "◦", "▪"}

// list renders the items of n, each after its bullet or number, its further
